Abstract: In recent times, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for legal case judgement summarization. Most prior works have tried traditional extractive and abstractive summarization of case judgements. However, hybrid or extractive-abstractive techniques have not been explored much. In this work, we propose a novel tree-of-thoughts inspired extractive-abstractive summarization approach for legal judgement summarization. We conduct experiments using two popular LLMs, DeepSeek and LLama, and compare among extractive, abstractive and extractive-abstractive summarization. Our experiments show that the proposed extractive-abstractive prompt provides better summaries compared to other types of LLM prompts.

</details>


### [36] [Can LLMs Judge Better Than They Generate? Evaluating Task Asymmetry, Mechanistic Interpretability and Transferability for In-Context QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28050)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在上下文QA任务中，自我评估并不比生成更容易；生成准确率在四个基准中的三个上超过自我评估，注意力分析显示评估时模型对上下文的关注度比生成时低3-5倍，且几乎不阅读候选答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM-as-a-Judge和自我评估流程中隐含的"评估比生成更容易"这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在受控的上下文QA设置中，以段落为唯一信息源，让模型自我评估自己生成的答案，消除参数化知识的混淆；结合注意力分析和LoRA微调实验验证。

Result: 在SQuAD 2.0、DROP、HotpotQA、MuSiQue四个基准上，生成准确率在三个基准上高于自我评估，仅多跳MuSiQue例外；评估时对上下文的关注度比生成时低3-5倍，且几乎不读候选答案；LoRA微调证实不对称性不是训练伪影。

Conclusion: 自我评估流程的核心假设存在缺陷，生成与评估之间存在不对称性，这挑战了现有LLM自我评估管线的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge and self-evaluation pipelines implicitly assume that evaluation is easier than generation. We test this in a controlled in-context QA setting where a context passage is the sole information source and each model judges the answer it generated, removing the parametric-knowledge confound of open-domain comparisons. Across four benchmarks (SQuAD 2.0, DROP, HotpotQA, MuSiQue) and two models, evaluation is not uniformly easier: generation accuracy exceeds self-evaluation on three of four, with multi-hop MuSiQue the exception. Attention analysis reveals why: evaluation attends to context 3--5x less than generation does and barely reads the candidate answer. LoRA fine-tuning confirms the asymmetry is not a training artifact: generation fine-tuning induces over-acceptance and evaluation fine-tuning degrades generation. These findings challenge core assumptions in self-evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [37] [MultiHashFormer: Hash-based Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28057)
*Huiyin Xue,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiHashFormer，通过哈希签名表示每个token，使基于哈希的自回归语言模型成为可能，在100M到3B参数规模上性能优于标准Transformer LM，并支持多语言词汇扩展而无需增加参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LM的嵌入矩阵随词表大小线性增长，参数开销大；现有哈希方法仅适用于编码器模型，因多对一冲突无法用于因果LM。本文旨在实现哈希在自回归LM中的高效应用。

Method: 每个token由多个独立哈希函数生成一个短哈希ID序列（哈希签名），Hash Encoder将签名压缩为潜向量供Transformer解码器处理，Hash Decoder生成下一token的哈希签名，再映射回文本。

Result: 在100M、1B和3B参数规模上，MultiHashFormer在多个基准上持续优于标准Transformer LM；支持多语言词表扩展而参数不变。

Conclusion: 哈希签名方法能有效突破嵌入矩阵的参数瓶颈，在保持自回归能力的同时实现参数高效，且天然适配多语言场景。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) represent tokens using embedding matrices that scale linearly with the vocabulary size. To constrain the parameter footprint, prior work proposes hashing many tokens into a single vector within encoder-only models. While this offers parameter efficiency, many-to-one collisions prevent its use in causal LMs. In this paper, we propose MultiHashFormer, a new framework that allows hash-based autoregression. Each token is represented as a unique hash signature, a short sequence of discrete hash IDs, generated by multiple independent hash functions. A Hash Encoder compresses this signature into a single latent vector for processing by a Transformer decoder. Then, a Hash Decoder generates the hash signature of the next token, which is then mapped back to text. We evaluate our approach at the 100M, 1B and 3B parameter scales, demonstrating that MultiHashFormer consistently outperforms standard Transformer LMs across multiple benchmarks. Furthermore, we show that our model handles multilingual vocabulary expansion with a constant parameter footprint without any modifications.

</details>


### [38] [Mechanism-Driven Monitors for Preemptive Detection of LLM Training Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28116)
*Ruixuan Huang,Yipei Wang,Wenyi Fang,Hantao Huang,Yifan Huang,Ansheng You,Zhenxing Zhang,Shuai Wang,Fan Wu,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于模块功能角色的机制驱动训练稳定性监测方法，通过监控QK双线性分解的谱熵和MoE路由器的专家选择指标，在损失发散前数千步即可检测到训练不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练消耗大量加速器集群和长时间计算，一旦发生数值或超参数故障，训练可能在损失和梯度范数看似正常的情况下持续数千步而不被发现，导致昂贵的稳定性故障成本。

Method: 从每个关键模块的功能角色出发，推导内部监测信号。对于低精度闪存注意力，监控QK双线性分解的谱熵（一阶项在损失完全崩溃前出现异常）；对于MoE路由器，推导基于专家选择角色的指标。

Result: 故障注入实验（低精度注意力、大学习率、组合故障）表明，这些信号为不同故障提供独特特征，可在损失发散前数千步触发警报。

Conclusion: 机制驱动的内部监测信号能够实现训练不稳定的早期检测，比传统基于损失或梯度范数的监测方法更早发现故障。

Abstract: Frontier large language model training consumes massive accelerator fleets and long wall-clock computation, making stability failures costly when they occur. After a numerical or a hyperparameter fault has already destabilized the training dynamics, it may continue for thousands of steps while loss and gradient norms still appear normal. We study mechanism-driven detection of training instability by deriving internal monitors from the functional role of each critical module and from the earliest computational sites where failures are expected to produce measurable signatures. For low-precision flash attention, we monitor the spectral entropy of a QK bilinear decomposition, whose first-order term becomes abnormal before the loss fully collapses. For MoE routers, we derive indicators from their role in expert selection. Our fault-injection experiments on low-precision attention, large learning-rate, and combined faults show that these signals provide distinct signatures for different failures, triggering thousands of steps before loss divergence.

</details>


### [39] [From Tokens to States: LLMs as a Special Case of World Models and the Continuous Path Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28127)
*Paul Dubois*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文反驳了"LLM预测token，世界模型模拟现实"的二元对立观点，论证LLM是世界模型的退化特例，并指出从NTP到JEPA存在连续谱系，同时讨论了沿此谱系移动带来的数据与架构两大开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳AI社区将LLM与世界模型视为二元对立的简化观点，提出更细致的理论框架来理解两者关系。

Method: 通过理论论证：1) 将LLM形式化为世界模型的退化特例（状态空间为所有token序列，动作为追加一个token）；2) 构建从NTP到JEPA的连续谱系，包含多token预测、未来摘要预测、下一潜在表示预测等中间态。

Result: 揭示了LLM与世界模型不是替代关系而是泛化关系，并识别出沿谱系移动时面临的两大挑战：从自监督文本到带标注动作环境的"数据悬崖"，以及Transformer对连续状态预测的适应性。

Conclusion: LLM与世界模型的关系是连续谱系而非二元对立；向世界模型方向移动虽能放松LLM约束，但会牺牲LLM可规模化训练的两大优势（自监督数据和Transformer架构），这两者构成未来的开放研究问题。

Abstract: The AI community has framed the relationship between large language models (LLMs) and world models as a dichotomy: LLMs predict tokens; world models simulate reality. Yann LeCun argues in 2022 that reaching general intelligence requires abandoning autoregressive token prediction in favour of latent-space architectures. This framing is unnecessarily binary. Two claims will be defended. First, LLMs are a degenerate special case of world models: the state space is the set of all token sequences, the only action is appending one token, and world models are therefore a strict generalisation of LLMs, not a replacement. Second, there is a natural continuous spectrum from NTP to JEPA, with multi-token prediction, future-summary prediction, and next-latent prediction as intermediate stations already populated by current research. Moving along this spectrum relaxes the LLM constraints one by one. It also progressively surrenders the two practical advantages that make LLMs trainable at scale: internet-scale self-supervised data, and a transformer architecture co-designed for discrete token prediction. Both are examined as open research questions: the data question (the cliff from self-supervised text to instrumented action-labelled environments) and the architecture question (whether the transformer generalises to continuous-state prediction, or whether a new primitive is needed).

</details>


### [40] [Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28186)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Xinyue Zeng,Zhuochun Li,Hong Jiao,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Epi2Diff框架，通过将大型推理模型(LRM)的推理轨迹映射为认知驱动的阶段序列，提取阶段动态特征并结合语义表示来预测人类试题难度。在四个真实数据集上，Epi2Diff优于强基线方法，在SAT分类基准上相比监督微调基线平均提升8.1%。分析表明，难题会诱发更费力、更迭代、更侧重实现过程的阶段动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有难度预测方法依赖昂贵的人工校准或文本特征，难以提供关于认知过程的证据。作者认为难度不仅是文本属性，更是问题求解负担的可观察结果，而LRM的推理轨迹能提供可扩展的过程证据，但需结构化以支持可解释建模。

Method: Epi2Diff框架将LRM推理轨迹映射为认知阶段序列，将轨迹片段分组为功能性的问题求解状态，从而通过推理规模、努力分配和状态转移来建模难度。提取精简的阶段动态特征，与语义项表示结合进行难度预测。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上Epi2Diff持续优于强基线（包括微调小语言模型、LLM上下文学习和监督LLM适配）。在SAT分类基准上相比监督LLM微调平均相对提升8.1%。分析发现难题诱发更费力、迭代、侧重实现过程的阶段动态。

Conclusion: LRM推理轨迹中的认知阶段为人类试题难度提供了可预测且可解释的过程表示，为教育测量中推理模型的应用开辟了新视角。

Abstract: Predicting human item difficulty is central to educational assessment, where reliable estimates support fairness and effective test construction. Existing methods often depend on costly human calibration or item-level textual representations, providing limited evidence about the cognitive processes that make items difficult. We argue that difficulty should be viewed not only as a property of item text, but also as an observable consequence of the problem-solving burden an item induces. Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) offer scalable process evidence through reasoning traces, but such evidence must be structured to support interpretable modeling. To this end, we introduce Epi2Diff (Episode to Difficulty), a framework that maps LRM reasoning traces into cognitively grounded episode sequences. These episodes group trace segments into functional problem-solving states, enabling difficulty to be modeled through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions. Epi2Diff extracts compact episode-dynamic features and combines them with semantic item representations for human difficulty prediction. Experiments on four real-world human difficulty datasets show that Epi2Diff consistently outperforms strong baselines, including fine-tuned small language models, LLM in-context learning, and supervised LLM adaptation. On SAT-derived classification benchmarks, Epi2Diff achieves an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised LLM fine-tuning baselines. Further analyses show that harder items induce more effortful, iterative, and implementation-centered episode dynamics, rather than merely longer responses. These results demonstrate that cognitive episodes in LRM reasoning traces provide a predictive and interpretable process representation for human item difficulty, offering a new lens for educational measurement with reasoning models.

</details>


### [41] [Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28273)
*Niclas Lietzow,Danielle Bitterman,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman,Michal Golovanevsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过激活修补、模型组件消融和机制分析，在三个VLM家族中发现了感知-知识冲突的稀疏因果回路：视觉接地默认发生，而先验知识接地依赖于集中在网络后半部分的一小组因果必要注意力头（2.5-4.8%），这些头可进一步分解为路由头和写入头。消融这些头可在68-96%的情况下将知识接地预测翻转为视觉接地答案，但对视觉接地预测影响极小（0.8-7.5%），揭示了不对称因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅从行为层面描述VLM在视觉证据与记忆知识冲突时的表现，缺乏组件级别的因果解释机制。

Method: 结合三种粒度的激活修补（残差流、注意力头、MLP子层）、模型组件消融实验和机制分析，对三个VLM家族进行系统性因果研究。

Result: 发现了VLM中感知-知识冲突的稀疏因果回路：视觉接地是默认行为，先验知识接地依赖于集中在后半网络的一小部分注意力头（2.5-4.8%）。消融这些头可使68-96%的先验知识预测翻转为视觉接地预测，而仅改变0.8-7.5%的视觉接地预测。这些注意力头可分解为路由头和写入头，该结构跨模型家族和规模一致。

Conclusion: VLM中存在一个稀疏的因果回路解释感知-知识冲突，其中先验知识接地依赖少量特定注意力头，且因果结构具有不对称性，这为理解和改进多模态系统的可靠性提供了组件级见解。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.

</details>

### [42] [OverFlowLight: Real-Time Gridlock Prevention and Traffic Signal Optimization for Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27381)
*Mingyuan Li,Boyang Huang,Tianqi Jiang,Chenpu Li,Chunyu Liu,Yang Li,Ruimin Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OverFlowLight是一个实时交通信号控制框架，通过多模态传感检测队列溢出并动态生成专用溢出相位，结合基于规则的快速干预和强化学习的长效优化，在实际部署中减少60.4%的溢出事件并提升18.2%的网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制算法主要优化通行效率，在高峰时段无法有效应对队列溢出问题，导致拥堵级联恶化及安全隐患。

Method: 利用相机和雷达的多模态感知实时检测溢出；检测后动态生成并插入专用溢出相位清除阻塞队列；采用混合控制设计，结合快速基于规则的溢出干预与强化学习等后端控制器以实现长期效率优化。

Result: 在三个主要城市的43个路口实际部署，与现有基线相比溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%；显著降低人工干预需求；可与现有基于强化学习的信号控制智能体无缝集成。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首个实用、可扩展、数据驱动的主动预防交通死锁框架，为构建韧性高效的城市交通系统提供了关键组件。

Abstract: Queue overflow, a severe consequence of urban traffic congestion, occurs when vehicle queues exceed intersection capacity, obstructing upstream traffic and triggering cascading gridlocks. Prevailing traffic signal control (TSC) algorithms, primarily optimized for throughput, often fail to address overflow during peak hours, exacerbating congestion and creating safety hazards. We propose OverFlowLight, a real-time framework designed to preemptively resolve overflow and enhance overall TSC performance. It first introduces a mechanism to accurately detect overflow in real-time by leveraging multi-modal sensing from cameras and radars. Upon detection, it dynamically generates and inserts dedicated overflow phases into the signal cycle to clear the blocking queues. This is orchestrated by a hybrid control design that combines rapid rule-based overflow intervention with controller back ends such as reinforcement learning (RL) for longer-horizon efficiency. We conducted extensive real-world deployments of OverFlowLight across 43 intersections in three major cities. The framework demonstrates seamless integration with existing RL-based TSC agents, highlighting its modularity and practical applicability. Empirical results show that OverFlowLight reduces overflow incidents by 60.4% and increases network throughput by 18.2% compared to deployed baselines. Furthermore, it substantially diminishes the need for manual intervention common with expert-tuned signal plans. This work presents the first practical, scalable, and data-driven framework for actively preventing traffic gridlock, offering a crucial component for building resilient and efficient urban transportation systems. Our demonstration videos, codes and datasets are available at the anonymous URL, https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OverFlowLight-FBF9.

</details>


### [43] [RANSAC Scoring Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27385)
*James Pritts,Felix Seegräber,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需手动设定内点尺度参数的RANSAC评分方法，通过共轭逆伽马先验解析边缘化内点尺度，在近7万图像对基准上超越现有方法，对阈值误校准鲁棒，且在小样本场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RANSAC变体需要用户提供依赖于内点尺度的阈值参数，而该尺度本身需从受污染数据中估计。作者希望消除这种对人工设定参数的依赖。

Method: 逆转传统推理顺序：在固定内点划分下，利用共轭逆伽马先验解析闭式边缘化内点尺度，然后优化划分。评分公式统一涵盖非信息Jeffreys极限和信息性经验贝叶斯先验，无需修改算法即可自适应数据丰富/稀缺场景。计算复杂度为O(N log N)，通过排序扫描实现。

Result: 在近7万图像对基准上，该方法在阈值误校准下保持几乎平坦的性能（基线方法显著下降）；仅需2个验证对即可达到近最优精度（基线需要约100倍以上）；在验证数据稀缺时自动收紧先验正则化。

Conclusion: 所提出的评分方法是首个公式中完全不含内点尺度参数的RANSAC评分，在多种双视图估计问题和特征管线中均超越现有方法。

Abstract: The most widely used RANSAC variants score candidate models by counting inliers or summing per-point scores that saturate beyond a residual threshold. Every such score requires a user-supplied parameter that is a function of the inlier scale, which must itself be estimated from contaminated data. We remove this dependence by reversing the usual order of inference: rather than estimating the scale and then scoring against it, we marginalize the inlier scale analytically in closed form under a conjugate Inverse-Gamma prior for a fixed inlier partition, then optimize over partitions. A single closed-form expression spans the non-informative Jeffreys limit and informative empirical-Bayes priors, so the same score adapts across data-rich and data-scarce regimes without any change to the algorithm. The proposed RANSAC score is the first in which the inlier scale is genuinely absent from the formula. The score admits O(N log N ) computation via sort-and-sweep. On a benchmark of nearly 70 000 image pairs spanning different two-view estimation problems and both engineered and learned feature pipelines, the proposed score exceeds the state of the art (RANSAC, MSAC, GaU, MAGSAC): it stays nearly flat under threshold miscalibration where baselines degrade, reaches near-optimal accuracy from as few as two validation pairs where baselines need ont he order of 100 times more,. and tightens its prior regularization as validation data grows scarce.

</details>

*Zhihao Dai,Dennis Bader,Alain Gysi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Darts库新增统一FoundationModel类集合，将Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM等基础模型集成到标准化接口中，实现零样本/微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，仅需修改模型名称即可无缝切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型通常以独立包形式发布，接口碎片化且与通用工具链互操作性差，导致联合评估和完整流水线集成困难。

Method: 在Darts中开发统一的FoundationModel类集合，为标准化的全周期预测接口提供最少外部依赖，集成Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM四种基础模型。

Result: 现有Darts流水线仅需更改模型名称即可使用基础模型；新流水线可实现零样本/微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，结合数据处理与评估工具，全部统一在单一框架内。

Conclusion: 通过统一接口将基础模型无缝集成到Darts生态，降低了基础模型使用门槛，促进了从训练定制模型到使用预训练通用预测器的范式转变。

Abstract: Since its initial release in 2020, Darts has become a widely used open-source Python library for time series analysis. A series of foundation models have recently claimed accuracy improvements in zero-shot forecasting, promising a paradigm shift from training custom models to harnessing pre-trained general-purpose forecasters. Foundation models, however, are often released as isolated packages with fragmented interfaces and limited interoperability with common tooling, making joint evaluation and integration within complete pipelines difficult. In Darts, we developed a unified $\texttt{FoundationModel}$ class collection (Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, TiRex, PatchTST-FM) that provides standardized, full-cycle forecasting interfaces with minimal external dependencies for integrating foundation models into the ecosystem. Existing Darts pipelines can now use foundation models with only a name change; new pipelines can use them for zero-shot or fine-tuned forecasting, uncertainty estimation, and backtesting, combined with data processing and evaluation tooling, all within a unified framework.

</details>

*Giosue Migliorini,Aristofanis Rontogiannis,Grigori Guitchounts,Nicholas Franklin,Axel Elaldi,Olivia Viessmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PairSAE方法，通过N-mode SVD压缩pairwise张量为token级交互角色，再用稀疏自编码器学习共享的token级特征，成功解决标准SAE在pairformer架构上特征二次爆炸和序列-表示联合分布问题，在Boltz-2蛋白质-配体复合物上获得可解释特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构生物学基础模型（如pairformer架构）的内部特征难以解释，标准稀疏自编码器在pairwise表示上直接应用会导致特征数量二次爆炸，且无法捕捉序列和pair表示中联合分布的概念。

Method: PairSAE首先通过N-mode SVD将高维pairwise张量压缩为token-wise的交互角色向量，然后使用稀疏自编码器学习一组共享的token级特征，这些特征可解码回序列和pair两种表示空间。

Result: 在Boltz-2模型处理PLINDER蛋白质-配体复合物的激活上，PairSAE学到的特征与UniProt注释对齐，并能用于预测Boltz-2的亲和力值。

Conclusion: PairSAE成功将结构生物学基础模型的潜在空间与可解释的结构概念联系起来，揭示了模型"知道"什么，同时避免了传统SAE在pairformer架构上的局限性。

Abstract: Foundation models for structural biology have achieved remarkable performance in predicting biomolecular structure and show promise for the design of proteins and small molecules. Yet understanding which internal features drive their outputs remains challenging. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs), effective on transformer-style sequence embeddings, do not transfer cleanly to pairformer-like architectures: naively operating on pairwise representations yields a quadratic blow-up of features and obscures concepts distributed jointly across sequence and pair representations.
  We introduce PairSAE, which summarizes pairwise tensors via an N-mode SVD into token-wise interaction roles, then uses a sparse autoencoder to learn a shared set of token-level features that decode into both sequence and pair representations. Evaluated on Boltz-2 activations for PLINDER protein-ligand complexes, PairSAE yields interpretable features that align with UniProt annotations and predict Boltz-2 affinity values. These results indicate that PairSAE links the latent space of foundation models for structural biology to interpretable structural concepts, clarifying what the model "knows" while avoiding pairformer-induced pitfalls that limit conventional SAEs.

</details>


### [46] [Learning in Markovian bandits with non-observable states and constrained decision epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27448)
*Thomas Hira,Victor Boone,Urtzi Ayesta,Ina Maria Verloop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了状态不可观测且决策时点可能受约束的马尔可夫赌博机中的遗憾最小化问题，聚焦于"纯策略"基准。提出了自退化马尔可夫赌博机模型，证明了在此类模型中纯策略总是渐近最优的。设计了UCB-NOM算法，在无先验知识时实现近对数遗憾，在具有先验知识时达到对数遗憾，且遗憾界不依赖于马尔可夫链的状态数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有马尔可夫赌博机研究大多假设状态可观测或允许任意切换策略，但实际场景中状态常不可观测且决策时点受限。作者旨在探索状态不可观测性对学习算法性能的影响，并设计在纯策略基准下有效的遗憾最小化算法。

Method: 提出自退化马尔可夫赌博机模型；证明在此类模型中纯策略渐近最优；证明无先验知识时稀有切换算法的遗憾必然超对数增长；基于UCB框架设计UCB-NOM乐观算法；利用偏差函数界作为先验知识实现对数遗憾。

Result: 无先验知识时，稀有切换算法的遗憾为ω(log(T))；UCB-NOM实现近对数遗憾；给定先验知识时UCB-NOM达到O(log(T))遗憾，最坏情况界为O(√(T log T))；所有遗憾界不依赖状态数。

Conclusion: 状态不可观测性在自退化马尔可夫赌博机中仅是一个轻微的困扰，通过适当算法设计可以克服，纯策略基准下的遗憾最小化是可行的。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of regret minimization in Markovian bandits with \emph{non-observable states} and possibly \emph{constrained} decision epochs. The focus is restricted to a ``pure'' regret benchmark, that compares the performance of the learning algorithm to the best \emph{pure policy} which -- akin to optimal policies of stochastic bandits -- picks the optimal arm from start to finish without ever switching. We introduce a generalization of rested Markovian bandits, \emph{self-degrading Markovian bandits}, for which pure policies are always asymptotically optimal.We show that without prior knowledge on the underlying bandit, the regret of algorithms that switch arms rarely necessarily scales super-logarithmically for every bandit, i.e., as $ω(\log(T))$, where $T$ is the learning horizon. Despite the unreachability of the logarithmic regime, we design UCB-NOM, an optimistic algorithm inspired by UCB, of which the regret is nearly logarithmic. Lastly, we show that given prior knowledge on the Markovian bandit in the form of a bound on the bias functions of its arm, a proper instantiation of UCB-NOM achieves $O(\log(T))$ regret. We further show that this prior knowledge allows for a $O(\sqrt{T \log(T)})$ worst-case regret bound for UCB-NOM. Notably, our regret bounds do not depend on the number of states of the underlying Markov chains. Our findings suggest that the non-observability of states is a mild inconvenience in self-degrading Markovian bandits.

</details>


### [47] [Prism Transformer: Progressive Head Schedules for Hierarchical Attention Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27449)
*Shubham Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prism Transformer通过逐层递增注意力头数（从少而宽到头多而窄）替代传统均匀分配方案，在不增加参数量和计算量的前提下，构建局部到全局的层级表示，显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多头注意力机制在所有层均匀分配隐藏维度（每头维度dh = dmodel/h），导致早期层头部的表示子空间过小，无法有效捕捉复杂的高维上下文模式，存在结构性瓶颈。

Method: 提出Prism Transformer架构，采用渐进式头部调度（progressive head schedule），随层数递增单调增加头数：浅层使用少量但更宽的头部捕捉局部组合模式，深层使用大量窄头部分解为专门化语言特征。该改变参数中性、计算中性，零训练/推理开销。

Result: 在124M、354M和757M三种模型规模下，Prism Transformer均一致优于均匀基线，验证损失持续降低，并在PIQA、HellaSwag、ARC-Easy、WinoGrande等零样本基准上持续提升。

Conclusion: 非均匀子空间分配能够在标准Transformer预算内释放潜在容量，更有效地利用模型能力。

Abstract: Multi-head attention conventionally partitions the hidden dimension equally across all heads at every layer, enforcing an identical representational subspace dimension (dh = dmodel/h) throughout the models depth. In this work, we identify this uniform allocation as a fundamental structural bottleneck: due to their restricted dimensional space, early-layer heads are unable to faithfully capture complex, high-dimensional contextual patterns. To resolve this, we introduce the Prism Transformer, a novel architectural paradigm that replaces the static, uniform head configuration with a progressive head schedule. By monotonically increasing the head count across layers, the Prism Transformer naturally establishes a local-to-global representational hierarchy: early layers leverage fewer, exceptionally wide heads to capture complex, local compositional patterns, while deep layers deploy many, narrow heads to decompose these patterns into specialized linguistic features. Crucially, this structural shift is parameter-neutral, compute-neutral, and introduces zero training or inference overhead, preserving identical weight matrices and FLOP budgets as the standard Transformer. Across three model scales (124M, 354M, and 757M), the Prism Transformer consistently outperforms uniform baselines, achieving consistent reductions in validation loss alongside consistent gains on downstream zero-shot benchmarks (including PIQA, HellaSwag, ARC-Easy, and WinoGrande). Our findings demonstrate that non-uniform subspace allocation unlocks latent capacity within the standard Transformer budget, enabling more effective use of model capacity.

</details>


### [48] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何条件傅里叶神经算子（FNO），用于学习二维平环面上三次非线性薛定谔方程的解算子。该模型以解的实部、虚部及纵横比参数ω²为输入，成功捕捉了有理环面与无理环面上不同的高频级联行为与Sobolev范数演化特征，消融实验表明显式几何条件输入能提升长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同纵横比的二维平环面上三次非线性薛定谔方程的动力学行为差异，探索利用几何条件傅里叶神经算子学习这类非线性色散偏微分方程中依赖几何结构的谱迁移现象。

Method: 构建几何条件傅里叶神经算子，输入为解的实部、虚部以及纵横比参数ω²，采用傅里叶伪谱法生成训练数据，训练模型逼近单步解算子。通过消融实验分析保留傅里叶模态数、激活函数、傅里叶层深度及显式几何条件的作用。

Result: 模型能够准确预测随机相位初始条件下两个环面上的解动力学，复现有理环面上更强的H²范数增长与无理环面上更受限的Sobolev范数行为。包含ω²作为输入可显著提升长期预测精度，尤其对有理环面效果更佳。

Conclusion: 几何感知的神经算子能够有效学习非线性色散偏微分方程中依赖几何结构的谱传递现象，支持将几何条件显式纳入模型设计以提升预测性能。

Abstract: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>


### [49] [The Curse of Multiple Mediators: Hidden Interaction Effects in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27510)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David Arbour,Aaron Mueller,Scott Niekum,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补（Activation Patching）的估计量，发现自然间接效应（NIE）不仅包含特定组件的因果效应，还包含交互效应（INT）。交互效应无法通过调整估计器或分析单位消除，且其大小与干净和修补激活之间的距离成比例，在局部仿射模型中可忽略。INT并非需要消除的干扰，而是可诊断因果结论是否依赖提示、以及贪婪的NIE-based组件排名是否会遗漏仅通过组合搜索才能发现的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视激活修补的因果解释，揭示其中被忽视的交互效应问题。

Method: 从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补估计量，在GPT-2 IOI电路上实验验证，并进行理论分析证明INT的性质。

Result: NIE包含INT；调整估计器或单位无法消除INT；INT导致因果重要性在条件依赖时被掩盖或膨胀；INT解释了 faithfulness 分数的不稳定性；INT在局部仿射模型中可忽略；INT可分解为成对和高阶交互。

Conclusion: INT不可避免但可作为诊断工具，用于判断因果结论的提示依赖性和贪婪排名的局限性。

Abstract: Activation patching is the primary tool in mechanistic interpretability. It attributes causal responsibility for a model behavior to each of its individual components by estimating its natural indirect effect (NIE). Re-deriving the activation patching estimand from causal mediation analysis, we find that the NIE does not solely capture the causal effect through the specific component. It also contains interaction effects (INT) that measure how much the component's causal effect itself depends on the state of other components in the model. A natural response may be to try to eliminate INT by adjusting the estimator or unit of analysis, but each of these potential remedies has predictable failure modes. We demonstrate these failure modes in the GPT-2 IOI circuit; components whose causal importance is conditional on the state of other components are either invisible or artificially inflated, and INT variance explains the previously documented instability of faithfulness scores. We prove that INT scales with the distance between clean and patched component activations, is negligible when the model is locally affine, and decomposes combinatorially into pairwise and higher-order group interactions. Despite its inevitability, INT is not a nuisance to be eliminated, but rather a diagnostic for interpretability studies. Its individual and group-level magnitude and sign signal when causal conclusions are prompt-dependent, and when greedy NIE-based component ranking will miss mechanisms only discoverable through combinatorial search.

</details>


### [50] [Boundary condition fidelity for bottom-hole pressure and CO2 plume prediction in geological carbon storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27515)
*Romal Ramadhan,Seyyed A. Hosseini,Larry W. Lake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了十种截断域边界处理方法对CO2地质封存中井底压力和CO2羽流预测的影响，发现保留角点孔隙体积最为关键，渐进修正因子结合传导率修正可在均质和非均质地层中实现最佳性能（BHP NRMSE < 3.7%，羽流IoU > 0.97）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CO2地质封存数值模拟中常采用截断计算域以降低计算成本，但人工边界会扭曲压力扩散和CO2饱和度分布，系统评估不同边界处理方法对预测精度的影响对于提高模拟可靠性至关重要。

Method: 对比十种截断域边界处理方法（包括均匀孔隙体积乘子、传导率修正、角点孔隙体积校正、分层校正及渐进修正因子等），以全域参考模型为基准，采用BHP RMSE、NRMSE、峰值压力偏差和羽流IoU作为评价指标，在均质和非均质储层中进行验证。

Result: 均匀处理方法导致BHP RMSE达250-382 psi，羽流IoU仅0.80-0.84；角点校正场景将压力误差大幅降低并使IoU提升至0.94以上；传导率修正并非普遍有益——均质地层中改善压力精度，非均质地层中可能过度限制流动；渐进修正因子结合传导率修正表现最优，BHP NRMSE低于3.7%，羽流IoU高于0.97。

Conclusion: 截断域建模中最重要的是保留角点孔隙体积；渐进修正因子结合传导率修正策略在不同储层类型中均能提供最稳健且最准确的预测结果，建议作为截断域模拟的推荐方法。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bottom-hole pressure (BHP) and CO2 plume migration is essential for safe geological carbon storage, yet practical simulations often rely on truncated domains where artificial boundaries distort pressure diffusion and CO2 saturation footprints. In this study, we evaluate how boundary-condition fidelity affects BHP and CO2 plume prediction by comparing ten reduced-domain boundary treatments against full-domain reference simulations in homogeneous and heterogeneous reservoirs. We test uniform pore-volume multipliers, transmissibility modifiers, corner-adjusted pore-volume corrections, layered corrections, and gradual modifiers using BHP RMSE, NRMSE, peak pressure deviation, and plume Intersection over Union (IoU) as performance metrics. Our results show that conserving corner pore volume is the most important requirement for truncated-domain modeling. We find that uniform treatments which neglect corner storage generate large pressure errors, with BHP RMSE of 362 to 382 psi in the homogeneous model and 250 to 304 psi in the heterogeneous model, and yield plume IoU values near 0.80 to 0.84, indicating roughly 16 to 20% of the combined plume area is misrepresented. Corner-adjusted scenarios substantially reduce pressure errors and raise plume IoU above 0.94, but we observe that transmissibility correction is not universally beneficial. In homogeneous reservoirs, uniform transmissibility adjustment improves pressure fidelity; in heterogeneous reservoirs, it can over-restrict flow across variable-permeability boundary faces, increasing BHP error and contracting the predicted plume. We find the gradual modifier with transmissibility correction provides the most consistent performance, achieving BHP NRMSE below 3.7% and plume IoU above 0.97 in both reservoir types.

</details>


### [51] [Productionized Fairness Measurement Under Privacy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27558)
*Osonde A. Osoba,Yuzi He,Saikrishna Badrinarayanan,Varun Mithal,Sakshi Jain,Natesh S. Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPRE方法，通过组合安全两方计算、差分隐私和加法同态加密等隐私技术，结合贝叶斯改进姓氏地理编码估计器和稀疏黄金调查数据集，实现对LinkedIn美国会员的种族/民族公平性测量，同时保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种族/民族等人口统计信号在法律上受限且文化敏感，难以获取和使用，导致公平性测量面临挑战，亟需一种隐私保护的方法来实现此类评估。

Method: PPRE方法将隐私技术（安全两方计算、差分隐私、加法同态加密）叠加在两种种族/民族人口统计信号源（贝叶斯改进姓氏地理编码估计器BISG和稀疏的自我报告调查数据集）之上，构建隐私保护的公平性测量解决方案。

Result: 详细阐述了PPRE的隐私保证，并在候选人侧和观众侧的公平性测量中展示了其应用效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可迁移的框架，供其他希望实施类似隐私保护测量基础设施的机构参考。

Abstract: Fairness measurements in the form of disaggregated evaluations often rely on demographic signals that are legally constrained or culturally sensitive. Race and ethnicity signals are among the more difficult signals to curate and use for this task. This paper presents Privacy-Preserving Probabilistic Race/Ethnicity Estimation (PPRE) as a method for enabling fairness measurements with respect to race/ethnicity for U.S.\ LinkedIn members in a privacy-preserving manner. PPRE applies privacy technologies (specifically: secure two-party computation, differential privacy, and additive homomorphic encryption) on top of two race/ethnicity demographic signal sources (the Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding estimator and a sparse golden survey set of self-reported demographics) to power a fairness measurement solution with respect to US-based race/ethnicity demographics. We detail its privacy guarantees and demonstrate its application on candidate- and viewer-side fairness measurements. We close with a transferable framework for institutions seeking to implement similar privacy-preserving measurement infrastructure.

</details>


### [52] [Quantum Generative Diffusion Model for Real-World Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27561)
*Jack Waller,Filippo Caruso,Dimitrios Makris,Rajagopal Nilavalan,Xing Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个量子生成扩散模型QDiffusion-TS，用于时间序列合成。通过用量子神经网络替换去噪Transformer中的前馈组件，构建了混合量子transformer架构，参数量减少近三个数量级。在苹果和亚马逊金融时间序列上，生成数据的Wasserstein距离比经典模型降低约44%，下游预测任务中RMSE提升高达71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型规模扩大带来计算成本与效率挑战，量子机器学习有望以更紧凑、高表达力的模型表示复杂数据分布，从而提升效率与可扩展性。

Method: 在经典扩散架构基础上，将去噪Transformer中的前馈组件替换为量子神经网络，构建混合量子Transformer；在IQM量子处理器上验证。

Result: 参数量减少近三个数量级；生成数据Wasserstein距离降低约44%；下游预测任务RMSE提升高达71%。

Conclusion: 量子增强架构能以显著更少的参数匹配并常超越经典模型性能，为高效可扩展的数据驱动生成建模提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable success in data synthesis, though recent advances driven by increasing model scale have introduced challenges in computational cost and efficiency. Quantum machine learning offers a promising alternative, representing complex data distributions using compact, highly expressive models. Here, we propose QDiffusion-TS, the first quantum generative diffusion model for time series synthesis, and validate it on the IQM quantum processor. The framework extends a classical diffusion architecture by replacing feed-forward components within the denoising transformer with quantum neural networks, yielding a hybrid quantum transformer that reduces the number of trainable parameters in each replaced component by nearly three orders of magnitude. Evaluated on financial time series from Apple and Amazon, the model generates synthetic data that more accurately reproduces the real distributions, reducing Wasserstein distance by approximately 44% relative to its classical counterpart across both datasets. In a downstream forecasting task, augmentation with the generated data improves predictive performance by up to 71% in RMSE over a baseline trained solely on real data. These results show that quantum enhanced architectures can consistently match and frequently surpass classical performance with substantially fewer parameters, establishing a practical framework towards more efficient and scalable data-driven generative modelling.

</details>


### [53] [hia-gat: A Heterogeneous Interaction-Aware Graph Attention Network For Frame-Level Traffic Conflict Risk Prediction On Freeways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27577)
*Mahshid Malazizi,Seyedmehdi Khaleghian,Mina Sartipi,Toru Hirano,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HIA-GAT，一种双流异构图注意力网络，将高速公路风险评估建模为图级二分类问题，利用物理信息边特征和冲突类型感知门控机制，在NGSIM数据集上实现了最佳风险排序性能（AUC 0.835/0.867），并提供可解释的逐车辆冲突类型归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于固定阈值（如TTC、PET）的风险评估方法难以捕捉复杂的交互关系，特别是横向（换道）冲突的图结构依赖未被充分建模，缺乏可解释的冲突类型归因能力。

Method: 构建每帧关系图（车辆为节点，同车道纵向/邻车道横向为两种边类型），引入物理信息边特征；提出HIA-GAT双流异构图注意力网络，通过独立注意力路径处理纵向和横向交互，并利用冲突类型感知门控机制（以SSM冲突归因为监督）融合两流输出。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集上，HIA-GAT平均AUC分别为0.835和0.867，在仅使用PET（横向冲突）的设置中增益最大；同时门控权重提供可解释的冲突类型归因。

Conclusion: 图结构对建模横向冲突风险至关重要，而纵向风险可通过非关系聚合捕获；所提方法支持可解释的实时高速公路安全监测。

Abstract: This paper formulates frame-level freeway risk assessment as a multi-agent scene graph-level binary classification problem, where each video or trajectory frame is labeled risky if any TTC- or PET-based conflict violates a specified severity threshold. We construct a relation-aware graph per frame with vehicles as nodes and two interaction types as edges: same-lane (longitudinal) and adjacent-lane (lateral), augmented with physics-informed edge features aligned to rear-end and lane-change conflict mechanisms. Building on a structured benchmarking suite of non-graph models and graph baselines, we propose HIA-GAT, a dual-stream heterogeneous graph attention network that processes longitudinal and lateral interactions through dedicated attention pathways and fuses them via a conflict-type-aware gating mechanism with event-level gate supervision derived from SSM conflict attribution. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 freeway datasets across nine TTC and PET threshold configurations show that HIA-GAT achieves the best average risk-ranking performance (AUC 0.835 on I-80 and 0.867 on US-101), with the largest gains on PET-only (lane-change) settings where relational structure is essential. Beyond accuracy, the learned gate provides interpretable per-vehicle attribution of dominant conflict type, supporting actionable, real-time freeway safety monitoring. We show that graph structure is critical for modeling lateral conflict risk, while longitudinal risk can often be captured by non-relational aggregation.

</details>


### [54] [PEBS: Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage for RLHF Reward-Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27578)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEBS是一种基于经验贝叶斯收缩的逐标注者仿射校准器，用于RLHF奖励模型中的标注者偏差校正，无需重新训练模型即可在PRISM和PluriHarms数据集上分别实现8.58%和9.66%的RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF奖励模型将所有标注者的偏好数据统一池化，拟合单一的全局仿射校准器，忽略了标注者之间系统性差异（如评分尺度的偏移和斜率不同），导致平均拟合结果无法代表任何单个标注者。

Method: PEBS为每位标注者独立拟合仿射校准器，并在保留的标注评分子集上应用Morris-James-Stein经验贝叶斯收缩，将其向总体均值收缩，以闭式解实现，无需重新训练奖励模型。

Result: 在PRISM数据集上，PEBS将用户内保留集RMSE相比池化总体斜率基线降低8.58%；在PluriHarms危害评分数据集上（Qwen-2.5基座模型，同族），RMSE降低9.66%。

Conclusion: PEBS是一种闭式后验估计器，用于RLHF奖励建模中标注者特定的仿射校准；它保持基础奖励模型不变，仅估计推理时用于新评分的标注者级映射。

Abstract: Reward models for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pool preferences across thousands of annotators and fit one global affine calibrator, collapsing raters with systematically different rating-scale offsets and slopes into a single average-rater fit that does not match any individual annotator. PEBS is a per-rater empirical-Bayes shrinkage estimator: it fits per-rater affine calibrators on a held-out slice of each annotator's ratings and applies Morris-James-Stein empirical-Bayes shrinkage toward the population mean, in closed form and without retraining the reward model. On PRISM, PEBS reduces within-user held-out RMSE by 8.58% over the pooled population-slope baseline. The procedure replicates on PluriHarms harm ratings (Qwen-2.5 base, in-family) with a +9.66% RMSE reduction over the same population-slope baseline. PEBS is a closed-form post-hoc estimator for annotator-specific affine calibration in RLHF reward modeling; it leaves the reward base model unchanged and estimates only the rater-level map used at inference time for new ratings.

</details>


### [55] [Difference of Convex Programming in the Wasserstein Space with Applications to MMD Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27767)
*Clément Bonet,Pierre-Cyril Aubin-Frankowski,Youssef Mroueh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将凸凹过程（CCCP）推广到Wasserstein空间，用于优化具有差之凸（DC）结构的目标泛函，并在MMD和能量距离上验证了算法的有效性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein空间上的许多目标泛函（如MMD）沿Wasserstein测地线是非凸的，标准一阶方法分析困难，需要更高效的优化框架。

Method: 将经典凸凹过程（CCCP）提升到Wasserstein空间，利用目标泛函的差之凸（DC）分解，在光滑性和强凸性假设下迭代优化。

Result: 在光滑强凸假设下证明了迭代的近似平稳性；为MMD和能量距离开发了显式的Wasserstein DC分解；实验表明合适的DC分解比Wasserstein梯度下降收敛更快更稳定。

Conclusion: Wasserstein CCCP为处理非凸Wasserstein优化问题提供了一种有效替代方案，在MMD等目标上具有理论和实践优势。

Abstract: Optimizing functionals over the space of probability measures is now ubiquitous in machine learning. A widely used approach is to perform the optimization directly over the Wasserstein space, but many objective functionals of practical interest are non-convex along Wasserstein geodesics, making the analysis of standard first-order methods challenging. In this work, we study a class of objectives over the Wasserstein space that admit a difference-of-convex (DC) decomposition and we lift the classical convex-concave procedure (CCCP) to this setting. Under smoothness and strong convexity assumptions on the convex components of the decomposition, we prove almost stationarity along the iterates of the resulting algorithm. Our main focus is on the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and the Energy Distance (ED) functionals, for which we develop explicit Wasserstein DC decompositions, and establish local convergence of the scheme under mild assumptions. Empirically, we show that well-chosen DC decompositions yield faster and more stable convergence than Wasserstein gradient descent on these MMD objectives.

</details>


### [56] [Retroactive Advantage Correction: Closed-Form V-Trace Bias Correction for Delay-Aware RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27580)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLHF中异步奖励信号问题，提出Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC)方法，通过队列缓存、非负核老化、裁剪残差注入实现延迟奖励的无偏或低偏优势估计，在tabular MDP上最高减少47.9x策略偏差，兼容PPO/GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RLHF经常面临异步奖励信号（如代码执行验证器、慢速评委集成、队列化人工审核），这些延迟奖励打破了标准PPO的同步奖励假设，导致策略偏差。

Method: 将每个未完成的慢速反馈进行队列缓存，通过非负核函数进行老化处理，然后作为裁剪残差重新注入到下一优化步的优势估计中。证明了在无偏裁剪重要性比率下，当有效延迟核完全注入时，累积RAC校正无偏；否则偏差与未注入部分成线性关系。在无延迟情景下退化为V-trace。

Result: 在tabular MDP概念验证中，双慢通道配置下RAC将策略偏差减少最多47.9倍，同时以更低的wall-clock成本优于wait-for-slow策略。

Conclusion: RAC通过两行补丁即可集成到PPO和GRPO的reward manager中，有效解决异步奖励下的策略学习问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in production does not always have a synchronous reward signal. Code-execution verifiers, slow judge ensembles, and queued human review can return several gradient steps after the rollout that produced them, breaking the synchronous-reward assumption underlying standard PPO. We address this gap with Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC): each pending slow completion is queued, aged through a non-negative kernel, and reinjected as a clipped residual into the next optimiser step's advantage. We prove that under an unbiased clipped importance ratio, the cumulative RAC correction is exactly unbiased when the effective delay kernel reinjects all of its mass, and carries a bias linear in the unreinjected fraction otherwise; at the no-delay identity kernel it reduces to V-trace. On a tabular Markov decision process (MDP) proof-of-concept, RAC reduces the closed-form policy bias by up to 47.9x at the two-slow-channel configuration, beating wait-for-slow at lower wall-clock cost. RAC integrates with PPO and GRPO through a two-line reward-manager patch.

</details>


### [57] [Dangerous Liaisons of Convex Learning and Non-Affine Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28123)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：梯度聚合操作要保持单调性，聚合规则必须是正仿射变换。非仿射聚合会破坏单调性，导致收敛不稳定和算法稳定性显著下降。作者提出了恢复单调性的充分条件，为理解现代学习系统中各类失败模式提供了统一理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶凸学习中，更新算子的单调性是保证最后迭代收敛性和泛化性的关键。线性平均能保持梯度更新的单调性，但现代机器学习流程中常采用非仿射梯度聚合（如自适应、隐私保护、鲁棒性、公平性等约束），这些操作会破坏单调性。作者希望回答：是否可能设计出保持单调性的非仿射聚合规则？

Method: 理论分析：证明梯度聚合的单调性保持当且仅当聚合规则是正仿射变换；量化非仿射聚合对收敛性和稳定性的损害；提出恢复单调性的充分条件。

Result: 证明了单调性保持的充要条件是正仿射聚合；非仿射聚合会阻止稳定收敛并显著降低算法稳定性；给出了恢复单调性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 非仿射聚合必然破坏单调性，从而损害学习系统的收敛性和稳定性；但通过识别充分条件可以在一定程度上恢复单调性。该理论框架统一解释了现代学习系统中观察到的各类失败模式。

Abstract: Last-iterate convergence and generalization guarantees in first-order convex learning hinge on the monotonicity of the update operator. While linear averaging preserves the monotonicity of gradient updates, this property is often violated when gradients are aggregated non-affinely, as in modern pipelines enforcing constraints like adaptivity, privacy, robustness or fairness. Whether it is possible to design non-affine aggregation rules that maintain monotonicity has remained an open question. We answer this question negatively: we prove that the monotonicity of aggregated gradients is preserved if and only if the aggregation rule is positively affine. Consequently, non-affine aggregation prevents steady convergence and substantially degrade algorithmic stability. We quantify these drawbacks and propose a path forward by identifying sufficient conditions under which monotonicity can be restored. Our results provide a unified theoretical framework explaining the disparate failure modes observed in modern learning systems.

</details>


### [58] [Global Explanations for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Models via $K$-Order Markov Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27599)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KARMA是一种通过构建马尔可夫代理模型来解释时间序列预测器的新方法，能够捕捉模型学习到的时间依赖关系，并通过五个层次的全局解释层级结构提供可解释性，在合成数据和真实数据上优于TimeSHAP等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法大多假设时间戳特征独立，忽略了时间序列预测中时间依赖和因果结构的基本特性，导致解释结果违反数据的时序和因果约束。

Method: KARMA包含三方面：(1)识别预测所需的最小历史长度K；(2)从离散化历史空间估计最佳拟合的K阶马尔可夫转移核；(3)从马尔可夫转移核导出五级全局解释层级结构。

Result: 在复杂合成数据上，KARMA能恢复模型学习到的因果结构，且在识别时间依赖关系方面优于TimeSHAP等现有归因方法。

Conclusion: KARMA有效解决了时间序列XAI中忽视时序依赖的问题，通过马尔可夫代理模型提供了更符合数据因果结构的全局解释。

Abstract: While many explainable AI (XAI) methods have been proposed, most are not designed for time-series forecasting models and often rely on the implicit assumption that timestamp features are independent. This assumption ignores the fundamental property of temporal dependence and can lead to explanations that violate the sequential and causal structure of the data. We introduce \textsc{KARMA}, a method for explaining time-series predictors by constructing a Markov surrogate model that captures the temporal dependencies learned by the predictor. Our approach revolves around three main aspects: identifying the minimal history length $K$ that is predictively sufficient for the model, estimating the best-fitting $K$-order Markov transition kernel from the discretized history space, and a five-level global explanation hierarchy that can be derived from the Markov transition kernel, which we illustrate using real-world weather data (Beijing PM 2.5). We also certify using complex synthetic data with known true causal edges that KARMA (i) recovers the data causal structure as learned by the model via a controlled experiment and (ii) identifies temporal dependencies better than established attribution methods such as TimeSHAP.

</details>


### [59] [Training Observable Control Policies to Expose Agent State Through Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27609)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,John J. Bird*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在通信受限条件下，通过观测智能体的动作来估计其状态的方法，利用强化学习训练策略以增强策略的可观测性，并在飞行器追踪问题中验证了该方法在最小化影响任务性能的同时提升了可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通信受限的多智能体系统中，智能体之间的显式通信不可用，但智能体与环境的交互动作仍可被观测。作者希望利用这些动作信息来估计智能体的状态，从而替代或补充缺失的通信。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，通过在训练奖励中引入可观测性激励，鼓励智能体学习生成更易于被观测者估计状态的动作策略；随后通过仿真对训练后智能体的策略可观测性进行分析。

Result: 在飞行器追踪问题中，该方法成功学习到了具有增强可观测性的策略，且该策略对名义任务性能的影响极小。

Conclusion: 在通信受限环境下，通过强化学习设计具有可观测性的策略是可行的，能够在不显著牺牲任务性能的前提下，利用动作信息实现对智能体状态的有效估计。

Abstract: Physical or operational constraints often impose communications limitations on autonomous agents. Such limitations complicate monitoring or multiagent coordination. Even when strong communications are absent, some information may still be available. The remainder of the relevant agent state may be reconstructed via estimation. The actions taken by an agent are a potential source of information -- as the agent interacts with the environment, these actions may be observed even in the absence of explicit communication. We investigate using actions to estimate the state of an agent, using reinforcement learning to develop policies which make the estimation problem more tractable. Policy observability is encouraged through the training reward and is analyzed using simulation of the trained agent. In an aircraft tracking problem a policy with enhanced observability is found that has minimal impact on nominal task performance.

</details>


### [60] [COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27611)
*Chuanhao Li,Xiaoan Xu,Dirk Bergemann,Ethan X. Fang,Yehua Wei,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COOPA是一个模块化LLM智能体架构，通过迭代置信度建模、元素级可解释性溯源和多求解器路由三大组件，在八个LLM骨干和四个基线中取得最优宏平均精度，最高比最强基线提升6.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学建模需要大量领域知识、数学抽象和求解器专业知识，现有LLM系统存在复杂问题精度低、输出不透明、求解器支持有限等局限。

Method: 提出COOPA架构，包含三个组件：(1) 迭代置信度建模——生成多个候选公式化表达，从多个维度自评，基于最大-最小置信度准则选择最优解；(2) 元素级溯源与置信度解释——将变量、参数、约束和目标函数链接到引用的源文本，提供可审计线索；(3) 多求解器路由——将问题路由到专门的优化智能体。

Result: 在三个OR基准、八个LLM骨干和四个基线中，COOPA在六个骨干上取得最优宏平均精度，比最强基线提升最高6.7个百分点。

Conclusion: COOPA通过模块化设计和可解释性组件有效提升了LLM在运筹学建模中的准确性和可信度，消融实验验证了迭代置信度建模的贡献，案例研究展示了溯源追踪和多求解器调度的价值。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) provides a rigorous framework for high-stakes decision-making, but effective OR modeling requires substantial domain knowledge, mathematical abstraction, and solver expertise. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet remain limited by low accuracy on complex problems, opaque outputs, and narrow solver support. We propose COOPA (COoperative OPerations Agent), a modular LLM-agent architecture for interpretable and scalable OR decision support. It combines three components: iterative confidence-based modeling, which generates multiple candidate formulations, self-evaluates them across modeling dimensions, and selects one using a max-min confidence criterion; element-level provenance and confidence explanations, which link variables, parameters, constraints, and objectives to quoted source text and provide an audit trail for human verification; and multi-solver routing to specialized optimizer agents for different OR problem classes. Across three OR benchmarks, eight LLM backbones, and four baselines under identical conditions, COOPA achieves the best macro-average accuracy on six of eight backbones and improves over the strongest baseline by up to 6.7 percentage points. A within-system ablation isolates the contribution of iterative confidence-based modeling, while additional analyses and case studies illustrate the value of source traceability and multi-solver dispatch.

</details>


### [61] [FoggyTrust: Robust Federated Learning with Hierarchical Trust Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27622)
*Emmanuel Rassou,Tomas Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOGGYTRUST提出了一种分层拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习框架，通过将信任计算本地化到雾节点，在保留FLTrust鲁棒性的同时，更好地处理全局异构数据，并在CIFAR-10上针对Krum和Trim攻击实现了比FLTrust超过50%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLTrust虽然通过服务器端根数据集引入信任评分来抵御拜占庭攻击，但在全局数据异构场景下存在信任估计的分布不匹配以及客户端漂移问题。

Method: 提出FOGGYTRUST，一种分层扩展FLTrust的框架，将信任计算下放至雾节点，在局部同构的客户端组内保持鲁棒性，并组合局部信任聚合与异构感知全局优化器（如FedAdam和SCAFFOLD）来同时解决分布不匹配和客户端漂移问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上针对Krum和Trim攻击实现超过50%的提升，并在真实野生动物监测数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 分层信任网络在异构、安全关键的社会影响力场景（如分布式野生动物监测）中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust federated learning seeks to protect distributed model training from malicious or corrupted clients without requiring access to their private data. FLTrust addresses this challenge by introducing a trusted server-side root dataset that assigns trust scores to client updates for more robust aggregation. In this work, we propose FOGGYTRUST, a hierarchical extension of FLTrust that localizes trust computation to fog nodes, allowing the framework to better handle globally heterogeneous data while preserving robustness within locally homogeneous client groups. We further show that this two-level architecture can simultaneously address distribution mismatch in trust estimation and client drift across groups by combining local trust-based aggregation with heterogeneity-aware global optimizers such as FedAdam and SCAFFOLD. Across benchmark datasets, FOGGYTRUST achieves its strongest gains on more challenging heterogeneous settings, particularly on CIFAR-10 under Krum and Trim attacks, where it achieves an over 50% improvement over FLTrust. We also test FOGGYTRUST in a real-world safari dataset to show the promise of hierarchical trust networks for robust federated learning in socially impactful, safety-critical settings such as distributed wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [62] [HybridCodec: Modeling Discrete and Continuous Representations for Efficient Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27627)
*Artem Ploujnikov,Francesco Verdini,Samir Sadok,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合离散-连续音频表示方法，结合时间压缩的离散token和降维的连续残差，通过混合编解码器和Transformer架构，在保持自回归推理优势的同时减少信息损失，实验证明在说话人特征保留和自回归步数缩减方面优于纯离散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散音频表示在多模态文本-音频系统和LLM集成中应用广泛，但离散化过程中的信息损失导致下游任务性能下降，亟需一种能减少信息损失的表示方法。

Method: 提出混合离散-连续焦点调制编解码器（hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec）和混合Transformer架构。该架构在离散域进行自回归推理，同时结合非自回归预测和连续残差上采样，实现离散token与连续残差的联合建模。

Result: 相比纯离散方法，该方法在说话人特征保留方面有显著提升，同时有效减少了所需的自回归推理步数。

Conclusion: 混合离散-连续表示策略能够有效缓解离散化带来的信息损失问题，在保持自回归建模优势的同时提升音频表示质量，为构建高性能多模态音频系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Discrete audio representations have become increasingly popular for building multimodal text-audio systems and integrating audio capabilities into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, numerous studies report performance degradation on various downstream tasks due to information loss during discretization. To address this, we propose a novel approach combining temporally compressed discrete tokens with dimensionality-reduced continuous residuals. Our framework consists of a hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec and a hybrid Transformer. This architecture performs autoregressive inference in the discrete domain, coupled with non-autoregressive prediction and continuous residual upsampling. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the retention of speaker characteristics compared to discrete-only methods, while simultaneously reducing the number of required autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [63] [Continual Learning for Sequential Personalization of Small Language Models: A Stability Monitoring Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27634)
*Thomas S. Paula,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究小语言模型在边缘设备上的顺序LoRA个性化微调，通过检查点级协议监控任务性能、遗忘和参考集漂移，发现轻量级参考集分布诊断能揭示模型特定不稳定模式，包括任务指标无法察觉的有害适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型部署于边缘设备时需要持续适应新数据，面临灾难性遗忘风险，现有基准（如TRACE）已显示持续微调会损害模型通用能力，因此需要研究SLM在持续学习场景下的稳定性监测方法。

Method: 采用顺序LoRA个性化微调SLM，在每次适应阶段后保存模型检查点，并在当前任务、先前任务和固定参考集上评估；使用轻量级参考集分布诊断分析模型稳定性。

Result: 轻量级参考集分布诊断能够揭示模型特定的不稳定模式，某些情况下任务级指标隐藏了有害的适应效应。

Conclusion: 检查点级监控协议和参考集分布诊断有助于发现SLM在持续学习中的稳定性问题，为未来的监测方法研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly being considered for deployment on edge devices such as laptops, enabling private, low-latency, and locally personalized applications. However, personalization requires models to adapt over time to evolving user- or task-specific data, placing them in a continual learning setting. This creates the risk of catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously learned tasks or broader model capabilities. Recent benchmarks such as TRACE have shown that continual fine-tuning can significantly degrade the general abilities of aligned large language models. In this work, we present a study for sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs. We save model checkpoints after each adaptation stage and evaluate them on current tasks, previously seen tasks, and a fixed reference set. This checkpoint-level protocol enables us to monitor task performance, forgetting, and reference set drift over time. We show that lightweight reference set distributional diagnostics can reveal model-specific instability patterns during sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs, including cases where task-level metrics alone hide harmful adaptation. We hope this can highlight new research avenues for monitoring stability of SLMs in a continual learning setting.

</details>


### [64] [TeRoR: Decoupled Temporal Rotation with Relational Circular Region for Temporal Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27651)
*Peijia Xie,Yike Liu,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeRoR是一种改进的时间知识图谱嵌入方法，通过将实体嵌入的时间演化进行划分，在复数向量空间中对头尾实体进行独立旋转变换来增强时序信息建模能力，并利用半径约束头实体在尾实体为中心的圆形区域内，从而有效捕捉关系的一对多、多对一和多对多映射特性，在四个数据集上取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeRo方法在建模关系映射特性（如一对多、多对一、多对多）方面表现不佳，同时在时序信息表达上也存在局限性。

Method: 提出TeRoR方法：1) 将实体嵌入的时间演化进行划分，在复数向量空间中对头尾实体进行独立旋转变换以增强时序信息建模；2) 训练一个半径参数，将旋转和平移后的头实体约束在以尾实体为中心的圆形区域内，以捕捉关系多样的映射特性。

Result: 在四个不同的TKG数据集上，TeRoR取得了与最先进模型相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: TeRoR有效解决了TeRo在关系映射特性和时序信息表达方面的不足，是一种简单高效且性能优越的TKG嵌入方法。

Abstract: In recent years, with the emergence of Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs), research on learning entity and relation representations in TKGs has attracted increasing attention, giving rise to a large number of TKG embedding methods. TeRo is a simple and efficient temporal knowledge graph embedding approach. However, TeRo does not do well in modeling the mapping properties of various relations, such as one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many. Meanwhile, it also has limitations in the expression of temporal information. To address these issues, we propose a novel TKG embedding method named TeRoR. This method divides the temporal evolution of entity embeddings, and conducts independent rotation transformations on head and tail entities in the complex vector space to strengthen temporal information modeling capacity. In terms of relational characteristics, we train a radius to constrain the rotated and translated head entities within a circular region centered on the tail entity, which effectively captures the diverse mapping properties of relations. Experimental results demonstrate that TeRoR achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models on four distinct TKG datasets.

</details>


### [65] [Are Time-Series Foundation Models Ready for E-Nose Data? An Empirical Assessment of Their Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27672)
*Taeyeong Choi,Mohammed Kamruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时序基础模型（TSFMs）在电子鼻（E-Nose）气体传感数据上的表现，发现微调是必要的，且将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示融合可进一步提升性能，揭示了当前TSFMs在气体传感应用中的潜力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时序基础模型（TSFMs）在多种时序任务和领域（如医疗、气候、制造）中展现了强大的泛化能力，但其在气体传感数据上的效用尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 选取Chronos-2和MOMENT等代表性TSFMs，在电子鼻（E-Nose）数据上进行系统评估。通过微调TSFMs并使用其嵌入表示，同时将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型学习到的表示进行融合，用于气体识别和浓度预测任务。

Result: 微调是TSFMs在E-Nose数据上取得满意性能的必要条件；将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示融合能进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前TSFMs在气体传感应用中既有潜力（通过融合可提升性能），也存在局限性（微调必不可少），为未来TSFMs在气体传感领域的研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Inspired by advances in natural language processing and computer vision, "time-series foundation models" (TSFMs) have recently been introduced with the promise of strong generalization across diverse time-series tasks, including forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection, as well as across domains such as healthcare, climate science, and manufacturing. However, their utility for gas-sensing data remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper systematically evaluates recent TSFMs on electronic nose (E-Nose) data. In particular, we investigate whether embeddings produced by representative TSFMs, including Chronos-2 and MOMENT, provide effective representations for gas identification and concentration prediction. Specifically, we show that fine-tuning is necessary to achieve satisfactory performance on E-Nose data, and fusing TSFM embeddings with representations learned by specialized predictive models can further improve the performance, suggesting both the potential and limitations of current TSFMs for gas-sensing applications.

</details>


### [66] [Textual Belief States for World Models: Identifiable Representation Learning Under Strict Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27681)
*Xiang Gao,Kaiwen Dong,Yuguang Yao,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Kamalika Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在部分可观测文本环境中通过严格潜在状态中介（strict mediation）来提升表示质量的方法。作者形式化了历史旁路（history bypass）导致表示质量不可测的问题，并提出了离散、可解释、变长的文本潜在状态，以及树结构强化学习方法 factorized GRPO (fGRPO)，在训练中强制执行严格中介。实验表明该方法在保持单步预测精度的同时，表示质量提升最高达57%， rollout 性能提升最高达98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于LLM的部分可观测环境中，现有架构因历史旁路（history bypass）导致潜在状态不可识别，表示质量与预测性能脱节，缺乏对潜在表示质量的有效度量与保证。

Method: 1) 形式化严格中介的必要性——证明严格中介使表示质量可经验测试，而历史泄漏架构破坏这一关联。2) 提出文本潜在状态——离散、可解释、变长表示。3) 提出 factorized GRPO (fGRPO)——一种树结构强化学习方法，在训练中强制实施严格中介。

Result: 在 TextWorld 和 ScienceWorld 基准上，严格中介方法在保持单步预测精度的同时，表示质量提升最高达57%，rollout 性能提升最高达98%，且增益随任务复杂度和时域长度增加。

Conclusion: 严格潜在状态中介在文本领域是可行且必要的，fGRPO 有效解决了离散文本潜在状态不可微分、LLM解码器容易绕过瓶颈的挑战，显著提升了表示质量和长程性能。

Abstract: World models in partially observed environments rely on latent representations that summarize interaction history, but in many modern LLM-based architectures predictive performance fails to reflect representation quality due to history bypass, rendering the latent state unidentifiable. Strict latent state mediation, requiring predictions to depend only on the latent state and action, is a classical principle that resolves this, but enforcing it in text-based settings is an open challenge: textual latent states are discrete and non-differentiable, precluding variational training, and expressive LLM decoders readily ignore the bottleneck. We show how to make strict mediation work in the text domain. We formalize why it is necessary, showing that strict mediation makes representation quality empirically testable while history-leaky architectures break this connection. We then introduce textual latent states, which are discrete, interpretable, and variable-length, and factorized GRPO (fGRPO), a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that enforces strict mediation during training. Experiments on TextWorld and ScienceWorld show preserved one-step prediction accuracy alongside up to 57\% gains in representation quality and 98\% improvements in rollout performance, increasing with task complexity and horizon.

</details>


### [67] [CBD: API-Only LLM Black-Box Unlearning through Controlled Behavioral Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27683)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Yijing Lin,Zhipeng Gao,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CBD，一种仅需API黑盒访问的机器遗忘框架，通过两个辅助模型创建受控行为分歧，利用梯度统计判别基实现目标数据与保留数据的高区分度，在无需模型参数和内部logits的情况下实现高效的LLM遗忘，显著优于11种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备通过API调用大语言模型时，边缘数据可能包含敏感、版权、有害或过时信息，需移除其影响。现有机器遗忘方法面临两大挑战：1) API-only黑盒访问下无法获取模型参数和内部logits；2) 目标数据和保留数据具有高度相似的提示结构或语义模式时，难以在遗忘同时保持模型在保留数据上的效用。

Method: CBD框架：(1) 使用两个辅助模型对保留输入和目标输入创建受控行为分歧；(2) 将行为分歧转化为遗忘相关性分数；(3) 根据分数将遗忘相关查询路由远离目标LLM。为提升高相似度下的区分精度，通过估计经验Fisher矩阵并求解正则化广义特征值问题，构建基于梯度统计的判别基，将遗忘信号引导至目标特定信息而非共享提示结构。

Result: 在ToFU forget10上，CBD接近重训练参考的遗忘效果，同时模型效用提升至74.90，比第二名基线高约15%；在WMDP上，将危险知识准确率降至25.68（接近随机猜测），同时保持MMLU准确率52.67。与11种白盒和灰盒遗忘基线相比，CBD实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡且性能波动极小。

Conclusion: CBD首次在仅API黑盒访问条件下实现了高效的LLM机器遗忘，通过行为分歧和梯度统计判别基有效解决了高相似度数据区分难题，在多个基准上达到或超越需要模型内部信息的白盒/灰盒方法，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Edge devices increasingly invoke large language models (LLMs) through API services for context aware edge intelligence, while edge generated data may be collected to improve LLMs and may introduce sensitive, copyrighted, harmful, or outdated information into model behavior. Machine unlearning offers a practical way to remove the influence of undesired data without retraining LLMs. However, existing methods still face two gaps. The first is API only black box access, where target model parameters and internal logits are unavailable. The second is how to preserve retained utility when unlearning target data and retained data share highly similar prompt structures or semantic patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Controlled Behavioral Divergence (CBD), an API only black box unlearning framework. CBD uses two auxiliary models to create controlled behavioral divergence between retained inputs and unlearning target inputs, converts this divergence into an unlearning relevance score, and routes unlearning related prompts away from the target LLM. To improve discrimination accuracy under high similarity between target and retained data, CBD constructs a gradient statistics based discriminative basis by estimating empirical Fisher matrices and solving a regularized generalized eigenvalue problem, guiding the unlearning signal toward target specific information rather than shared prompt structures. Compared with eleven white box and gray box unlearning baselines, CBD achieves a better unlearning utility trade off and its performance varies little across settings. On ToFU forget10, CBD approaches the retrained reference on the forget set while raising model utility to 74.90, about 15% above the second best baseline. On WMDP, it lowers hazardous knowledge accuracy to 25.68, near random guessing, while preserving MMLU accuracy of 52.67. Code is at https://github.com/DGL-codes/CBD.

</details>


### [68] [Deployment-Side Adaptiveness in Multi-Horizon Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27688)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多步波动率预测中，同一训练好的多输出（MIMO）模型通过改变推理时的 rollout 规则可以生成不同精度和成本的预测族；验证集选择的规则能低代价改善默认 MIMO，且政策排名对评估指标（MSE vs QLIKE）敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测中，预测性能不仅取决于训练什么模型，还取决于如何部署训练好的模型。本文研究多步波动率预测中部署策略的影响。

Method: 以训练好的多输出（MIMO）预测器为基础，通过改变推理时的 rollout 规则生成一族预测；在20个股指波动率序列、三个预测步长、多种架构（线性模型到 PatchTST）上，评估基于验证集的部署策略。

Result: 非默认 rollout 规则常优于标准 MIMO 部署；最佳固定规则因架构和步长而异；验证集选择的单规则能以低成本改进默认 MIMO；小规则子集能接近大集成的大部分收益；MSE 选择的策略无法一致迁移到 QLIKE 损失。

Conclusion: 推理时部署是金融预测中一个有意义的自适应来源；训练好的波动率预测器应同时评估架构和部署策略。

Abstract: In financial forecasting, predictive performance depends not only on which model is trained, but also on how the trained model is deployed. We study this issue in multi-horizon volatility forecasting. Our starting point is that a trained multi-output (MIMO) forecaster does not define a single deployable predictor: by changing the inference-time rollout rule, the same trained model induces a family of forecasts with different accuracy and cost profiles. Across 20 stock-volatility series, three forecast horizons, and architectures ranging from linear models to PatchTST, we find that non-default rollout rules often improve over standard MIMO deployment. However, the best fixed rule varies substantially across architectures and horizons, making any single static replacement unreliable. We therefore evaluate validation-based deployment policies over the induced rule family. Under the primary MSE objective, validation-selected singletons provide a low-cost improvement over default MIMO, while small rule subsets recover much of the benefit of larger ensembles at substantially lower inference cost. We also find that policy rankings are metric-sensitive: MSE-selected policies do not transfer uniformly to QLIKE, a finance-standard volatility loss. These results show that inference-time deployment is a meaningful source of adaptiveness in financial forecasting, and that trained volatility forecasters should be evaluated not only by their architecture, but also by their deployment policy.

</details>


### [69] [Halt Fast! Early Stopping for Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27694)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于元学习的任意时间有效认证鲁棒性框架，通过轻量级元学习器预测图像特定的先验，用于序列E过程，在保持严格统计保证的同时，将样本复杂度降低了20倍，并实现了基于风险阈值的自适应计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准随机平滑方法需要数万次模型评估且需预先固定样本量，计算成本极高，限制了其在实际部署中的应用。

Method: 提出元学习框架：使用轻量级元学习器预测图像特定的先验，结合序列E过程实现任意时间有效的认证，自适应地分配计算资源。

Result: 相比传统方法，样本复杂度降低20倍；在保持相似认证性能的同时，实现了基于应用特定风险阈值的自适应计算分配。

Conclusion: 该方法为实时、安全关键场景下的认证部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) provides rigorous robustness guarantees for neural networks without architectural constraints, yet its adoption is limited by extreme computational costs. Standard RS requires tens of thousands of model evaluations per input and forces practitioners to commit to fixed sample sizes a priori. In this work, we present a novel meta-learning framework for anytime-valid certified robustness that adaptively deploys computational resources. By using a lightweight meta-learner to predict image-specific priors for a sequential E-process, we achieve a 20-fold reduction in sample complexity compared to traditional methods while maintaining rigorous statistical guarantees. Beyond raw efficiency, we demonstrate how anytime-validity enables adaptively allocating compute based upon application-specific risk thresholds, a form of resource triage impossible under classic certification frameworks. That this is achievable while also providing similar certification performance demonstrates that our approach provides a pathway for real-time, safety-critical certification deployments.

</details>


### [70] [Class-frequency Guided Noise Schedule for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27696)
*Jiequan Cui,Beier Zhu,Qingshan Xu,Xiaojuan Qi,Bei Yu,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次探讨了扩散模型中类别频率与多尺度噪声调度之间的相关性，发现低频类别因更大的低密度区域导致分数估计不准确，而高频类别主导分数空间。为此提出类别频率引导的噪声调度（CFRG），为低频类别分配更大尺度噪声，在不平衡数据集上显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分数生成模型中，低密度区域会导致分数估计不准确，从而影响生成质量。虽然多尺度噪声调度可以缓解该问题，但低频类别仍面临更大的低密度区域挑战，且高频类别主导分数空间，导致生成的样本在低频类别上质量和多样性不足。

Method: 提出类别频率引导的噪声调度（Class-frequency Guided Noise Schedule, CFRG），核心思想是为低频类别分配更大尺度的噪声，以补偿其较大的低密度区域带来的分数估计不准确性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT等不平衡数据集上，针对图像生成、图像分类和文本到图像生成任务，CFRG噪声调度相较于基线方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 频率统计信息在噪声调度设计中起着关键作用，CFRG方法有效缓解了类别不平衡导致的生成质量和多样性问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we are the first to examine the correlations between class frequency and the multi-scale noise schedule within diffusion models. For score-based generative models, low-density regions often lead to inaccurately estimated scores, thereby compromising the generation quality. Although the multi-scale noise schedule can alleviate this issue during the diffusion process, low-frequency classes still face the challenge of large low-density regions, resulting in more inaccurate estimated scores than high-frequency classes. Furthermore, high-frequency classes tend to dominate the score space, causing a convergence of most data points towards generating samples from these classes. Consequently, samples generated within low-frequency classes exhibit suboptimal quality and limited diversity. To address this challenge, we propose the \textit{Class-frequency Guided (CFRG)} noise schedule, leveraging the insight that low-frequency classes should be endowed with larger-scale noises. To illustrate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct experiments on various tasks, including image generation, image classification, and text-to-image generation, using imbalanced datasets, \textit{i.e.}, CIFAR-100-LT, and ImageNet-LT. By employing the CFRG noise schedule, we achieve substantial improvements over baselines, manifesting the crucial role of frequency statistics in noise schedule design.

</details>


### [71] [What Was That Again? Certified Robustness for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27698)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil Marchant,Jiani Xie,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于认证机制的语音识别安全增强方法，通过双门诊断流水线（双边原子审计+排名竞赛）在无需真实转录先验知识的情况下，实现高达55%的词错误率相对降低，同时提供词级和句级认证以增强声学安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别系统对对抗性和良性扰动高度敏感，但在部署系统中由于缺乏真实转录的先验知识，检测这类行为极为困难。

Method: 提出双门诊断流水线：(1) 双边原子审计（Two-Sided Atomic Audit），通过累积统计财富来认证token的存在性和排除对抗性干扰；(2) 基于排名的竞赛（Rank-Based Tournament），选择胜出的序列。

Result: 在四种不同架构上的评估表明，词错误率（WER）相对降低高达55%，同时召回率提升，置信度与WER之间的Spearman相关性降低，并提供了词级和句级的细粒度认证。

Conclusion: 认证启发的机制能有效提升ASR系统的声学安全性，在无需真实转录先验知识的情况下显著降低WER并提供可解释的认证保障。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition systems are notoriously both sensitive to adversarial and benign perturbations. While this has been repeatedly demonstrated using reference datasets, detecting such behaviors in deployed systems is incredibly challenging, due to the absence of oracle knowledge of the true transcription. We demonstrate that employing a certification-inspired mechanism can significantly decrease WER, increase recall, and decrease the Spearman correlation between confidence and WER. We achieve this through a dual-gate diagnostic pipeline: a Two-Sided Atomic Audit that accumulates statistical wealth to certify both token existence and adversarial exclusion, and a Rank-Based Tournament that selects the winning sequence. Our evaluations across four diverse architectures demonstrate up to a 55% relative reduction in Word Error Rate, while also providing granular word- and sentence-level certifications to enhance acoustic security.

</details>


### [72] [The Simulacrum: Decision-Theoretic Pretraining for Near-Optimal Time-Series Forecasting and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27711)
*Pablo Montero-Manso,Marcel Scharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于决策理论预训练的神经网络框架，通过在生成性世界中进行分层模拟训练，使神经估计器能够近似最优决策规则，在零样本推理中实现超越传统方法的预测、参数估计和模型选择性能，并成功解决了AR模型有限样本偏差和预测组合难题等长期挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列估计方法（如MLE、AICc）在处理有限样本偏差、复杂结构方程或计算密集型最优性准则时面临分析上难以处理或计算上不可行的困境，亟需一种能够直接逼近过程级有限样本性质、且允许分析师明确控制决策-理论权衡的通用框架。

Method: 分析师首先指定一个生成性世界（数据生成过程的分布）和目标决策目标，然后在该生成性世界的分层模拟上训练神经网络，使其逼近对应的最优决策规则，从而得到神经估计器。该框架支持零样本推理，可直接生成预测、参数估计、预测区间或模型选择结果。

Result: 实验表明：（1）神经估计器在相同结构模型类上优于MLE和AICc等传统基线；（2）即使仅基于结构模型模拟训练，在真实世界基准上也能达到与统计模型、神经网络或大型预训练模型竞争甚至领先的预测精度；（3）成功解决了AR(p)模型有限样本偏差/校准问题以及预测组合难题。

Conclusion: 该框架的核心优势在于能够逼近传统方法难以求解的时间序列问题（如复杂结构方程或最优性准则），通过显式控制决策-理论权衡，为分析师提供高度定制化且高效的估计工具，尤其适用于有限样本和计算受限场景。

Abstract: We introduce a neural network-based framework for learning time series estimators through a process we term decision-theoretic pretraining. Analysts specify a generative world, a distribution over data-generating processes, and a target decision objective. A neural network trained on stratified simulations from this world approximates the corresponding optimal decision rule, yielding a neural estimator that provides forecasts, parameter estimates, predictive intervals, or model-selection for zero-shot inference on previously unseen time series.
  The joint specification of the generative world and objective enables the estimators to directly approximate process-level, finite-sample properties: near-optimal risk, bias control, minimax performance, and uniform calibration. Our experiments demonstrate that these neural estimators can outperform traditional baselines such as maximum likelihood estimation and model selection via AICc, for the same model structural model classes. Furthermore, even when trained purely on simulations of structural models, they achieve competitive or state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy on major real-world benchmarks, compared with statistical, neural or large pre-trained models.
  We illustrate the framework by addressing two longstanding challenges: finite-sample bias and miscalibration in AR(p) models, and the forecast combination puzzle. These applications highlight the approach's main advantage: its ability to approximate solutions to analytically intractable or computationally prohibitive time series problems, including complex structural equations or optimality criteria. Ultimately, by enabling explicit control over decision-theoretic trade-offs, the framework equips analysts with highly efficient estimation tools tailored to their specific analytical needs.

</details>


### [73] [Aurora: A Leverage-Aware Spectral Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27715)
*Alec Dewulf,Dhruv Pai,Li Yang,Ashley Zhang,Ben Keigwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Muon优化器在矩阵参数更新中存在行范数不均匀问题，导致神经元更新停滞。提出Aurora优化器，在保持Muon极分解几何特性的同时强制行均匀更新，在预训练实验中优于Muon，且与现有方法结合后达到谱优化器最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在处理高矩阵（如MLP层投影矩阵）时，行范数会出现任意不均匀性，导致自强化反馈循环：部分神经元持续接收微小更新，最终对网络输出无实质贡献。现有缓解方法（如行归一化）会偏离Muon更新几何中动量矩阵的极分解因子，这是不期望的。

Method: 提出Aurora优化器，在保持Muon极分解几何特性的前提下，强制执行矩阵参数更新的行均匀性。通过额外的行归一化步骤约束更新方向，同时保留Muon的极因子几何结构。

Result: Aurora在预训练实验中优于Muon；与现有方法结合后，在modded-nanoGPT速度跑的优化器赛道中达到谱优化器最先进水平；Aurora相对于Muon的实证增益随MLP扩展因子增大而增加。

Conclusion: Aurora通过几何感知的行均匀化策略有效解决了Muon的行范数不均匀问题，且可能支持极宽MLP层的有效训练。

Abstract: We show that for tall matrix parameters, like projection matrices in the MLP layers, the Muon update can have row norms that are arbitrarily non-uniform. This can lead to a self-reinforcing feedback loop whereby neurons receive persistently small updates and eventually do not contribute meaningfully to network outputs. This problem is effectively mitigated by an additional row normalization step, but current methods do this in a way that moves the Muon update geometry away from the polar factor of the momentum matrix, which we find is undesirable. We propose Aurora, an optimizer that enforces row-uniformity of matrix parameter updates while respecting Muon's polar factor geometry. Aurora outperforms Muon in our pre-training experiments and, when combined with existing methods, achieves state-of-the-art performance among spectral optimizers on the optimizer track of the modded-nanoGPT speedrun.
