Method: 1) 理论刻画二元逻辑斯蒂模型中Fisher信息量与预测熵之间的权衡关系；2) 提出一个概率因果框架生成具有瞬态边和已知真实因果结构的时序图；3) 推导二元逻辑斯蒂参数化下的Cramér-Rao下界，并在合成数据上验证估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡。

Result: 在参数恢复最优的区间（高Fisher信息量）反而对应最高预测熵，导致即使参数估计精准，预测不确定性仍然最大；验证了估计误差与不可约预测损失之间存在明确的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 预测准确率单独不足以衡量模型是否学到了因果机制，未来的基准测试应区分可约减的模型误差与过程固有的不确定性。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is usually evaluated by predictive performance on unseen edges, but in probabilistic temporal graphs this criterion can conflate model error with irreducible uncertainty. We study this issue by characterising an inherent estimation--prediction tradeoff in binary logistic models where regimes that maximise Fisher information and improve parameter recoverability are also those with the highest entropy, making individual predictions intrinsically harder even under perfect parameter recovery. We propose a probabilistic causal framework for generating temporal graphs with transient edges and known ground-truth causal structure, allowing temporal link prediction to be evaluated jointly with causal parameter recovery. For the proposed binary logistic parametrisation, we derive the Cramér--Rao bound and validate the tradeoff between parameter estimation error and irreducible predictive loss. Our results show that predictive accuracy alone may not reflect whether a model has learned the underlying causal mechanism, motivating benchmarks that distinguish reducible model error from intrinsic process uncertainty.

</details>


### [112] [Disentangling Continuous-Time Latent Dynamics: Identifiability of Latent SDEs via Diffusion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28228)
*Yuanyuan Wang,Wenjie Wang,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间潜变量随机微分方程（SDE）中因果表示的可识别性问题，通过利用环境诱导的扩散协方差变化，证明了在无需漂移稀疏性假设的条件下，潜变量坐标及其因果图可识别到置换和缩放程度，并提出了两阶段估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间潜变量因果模型已建立较强的可识别性结果，但连续时间潜变量SDE模型的可识别性仍基本未解决。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 研究加性噪声潜SDE模型，观测数据通过未知非线性微分同胚映射得到，漂移项共享但扩散协方差随环境变化。理论证明：若存在两个对角扩散机制且坐标方差比两两不同，则潜变量坐标可识别到置换和缩放。先从线性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck系统证明，再推广到一般加性噪声潜SDE。在温和光滑性条件下，瞬时漂移-雅可比因果图可识别到相同置换。提出两阶段估计器实现潜变量解缠和可选因果图恢复。

Result: 理论证明了在连续时间SDE模型中潜变量坐标和因果图的可识别性；合成系统实验验证了理论预测的可识别性边界；Hardanger Bridge监测数据的实际应用展示了方法在真实传感器轨迹上的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次在连续时间潜变量SDE框架下建立了基于扩散协方差环境变异的可识别性理论，为实际连续时间序列数据的因果表示学习提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time series has developed strong identifiability results in discrete-time latent causal models, but identifiability in continuous-time latent stochastic differential equation (SDE) models remains largely open. We address this gap using environment-induced shifts in diffusion covariance. We study additive-noise latent SDEs observed through an unknown nonlinear diffeomorphism, with shared drift but environment-specific diffusion covariance. We show that two diagonal diffusion regimes with pairwise distinct coordinate-wise variance ratios identify the latent coordinates up to permutation and scaling, without any sparsity assumption on the drift. We first prove this result for linear Ornstein--Uhlenbeck systems and then extend it to general additive-noise latent SDEs. Under mild smoothness, the instantaneous drift-Jacobian causal graph is identifiable up to the same permutation. We propose a two-stage estimator for latent disentanglement and optional graph recovery; experiments on synthetic systems confirm the predicted identifiability boundary, and an application to Hardanger Bridge monitoring data illustrates the approach on real sensor trajectories.

</details>


### [113] [How Width and Data Shape Generalization Scaling Laws in Quadratic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28242)
*Julius Girardin,Emanuele Troiani,Yizhou Xu,Vittorio Erba,Florent Krzakala,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个二次两层网络在有限样本和结构化数据下的泛化误差，揭示了泛化误差随参数量、样本量和正则化变化的相位图，并发现了由目标谱结构控制的依赖于数据的幂律缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律的理论工作通常在固定特征或无限宽体系下研究泛化与数据或计算量的关系，而本文希望在特征学习模型中同时研究泛化如何随可训练参数数量和样本数量缩放，以填补这一理论空白。

Method: 分析了一个$\ell_2$正则化的二次两层网络在有限样本设置下的经验测试误差最小化问题，通过显式刻画泛化误差与样本数、模型宽度和正则化的函数关系，推导出不同缩放阶段的相位图。

Result: 发现泛化误差遵循由目标谱结构控制的数据依赖幂律规律；刻画了不同参数数量下的不同缩放阶段，包括内插起始点及其对泛化的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供了在特征学习模型中关于模型大小和数据规模如何共同影响泛化的理论理解，揭示了相变行为及不同缩放阶段的特征。

Abstract: Understanding how performance scales jointly with model size and data is a central problem in modern machine learning. Existing theoretical works on scaling laws typically describe generalization as a function of data or compute, often in fixed-feature or infinite-width regimes and for online SGD. Here, we instead study how generalization scales with the number of trainable parameters and the number of samples in a feature-learning model. We analyze $\ell_2$-regularized empirical test error minimization in a quadratic two-layer network in a finite-sample setting with structured data. This setting allows for an explicit characterization of the generalization error as a function of the number of samples, model width, and regularization. Our results reveal a phase diagram with distinct scaling regimes as the number of parameters varies. In particular, the generalization error follows data-dependent power laws controlled by the spectral structure of the target. We further characterize the transitions between regimes, including the onset of interpolation, and their impact on generalization.

</details>


### [114] [Parameter Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT) for Network Traffic Prediction via Dynamic Urban Congestion Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28274)
*Abdolazim Rezaei,Mehdi Sookhak,Mahboobeh Haghparast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数高效的混合Transformer（PEHT）网络流量预测框架，通过LoRA减少参数量，融合城市移动性和拥塞信息，在米兰数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态城市蜂窝网络中，网络流量预测对资源分配至关重要，但受复杂移动模式、拥塞动态和异构用户行为影响，预测极具挑战性。

Method: PEHT框架将主要网络通信特征与次要城市移动性特征分离，在Transformer编码器中引入LoRA减少可训练参数，并通过多模态融合策略将外部移动性和拥塞特征注入解码器以提升预测。

Result: 在Telecom Italia Milan数据集和多个合成拥塞场景下，PEHT在RMSE、MAE和R²指标上均优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: PEHT通过参数高效的设计和多模态融合，有效提升了城市蜂窝网络流量预测的精度和效率。

Abstract: Accurate network traffic prediction is a critical element for efficient resource allocation in dynamic urban cellular networks. However, prediction remains challenging because network demand is influenced by complex mobility patterns, congestion dynamics, and heterogeneous user behavior. This paper introduces the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT), a network traffic prediction framework that integrates urban mobility and congestion information into a Transformer-based architecture. PEHT separates primary network communication features from secondary urban mobility features and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into the Transformer encoder to reduce the number of trainable parameters while maintaining high predictive accuracy. A multimodal fusion strategy then injects external mobility and congestion features into the decoder to improve traffic forecasting. Experiments on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and multiple synthetic congestion scenarios show that PEHT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of RMSE, MAE, and $R^2$. The implementation is available in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [115] [Democratic ICAI: Debating Our Way to Steering Principles from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28294)
*Kevin Kingslin,Anish Natekar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Savita Bhat,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Democratic ICAI通过结构化角色辩论生成多竞争性理由，从偏好中提取更丰富的自然语言原则，从而提升对齐的可解释性和偏好预测的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于偏好的对齐方法（如ICA）仅通过单次解释捕捉决策中的细微差别，忽视了人类判断中多个交互标准的复杂性，导致可解释性不足。

Method: 提出Democratic ICAI，通过结构化角色辩论收集多个竞争性理由，从更丰富的信号中推导出更清晰全面的指导原则，并用于LLM和决策树评判者的决策建模。

Result: 在MuCE-Pref和LiTBench的多个创意任务类别上，Democratic ICAI产生了更忠实的偏好结构，在平均偏好预测上优于深思熟虑式提示和基于原则的基线方法，且LLM标注者更偏好其生成的准则。

Conclusion: Democratic ICAI通过多视角辩论机制有效提升了复杂决策场景下偏好对齐的可解释性和预测准确性。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment often struggles to capture the reasoning that underlies human judgments. Many evaluations rely on multiple interacting criteria, yet pairwise labels reveal only the final choice rather than the considerations that shape preferences. Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) improves interpretability in decision making by summarizing preferences into natural-language principles, but its single-pass explanations miss much of the nuance involved in complex decisions. We introduce Democratic ICAI, a novel approach that gathers multiple competing rationales through structured persona debate, offering a broader and more expressive account of the factors influencing each comparison. From these richer signals, we derive clearer and more comprehensive steering principles and use them to guide decision modeling through both LLM-based and decision-tree judges. Experiments on creative preference benchmarks, MuCE-Pref and LiTBench, across multiple creative task categories show that Democratic ICAI yields a more faithful preference structure. It improves average preference prediction across tasks relative to deliberative prompting and principle-based baselines, while producing constitutions that LLM annotators prefer.

</details>


### [116] [VGB for Masked Diffusion Model: Efficient Test-time Scaling for Reward Satisfaction and Sample Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28301)
*Kijung Jeon,Thuy-Duong Vuong,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDM-VGB，一种基于掩码扩散模型（MDM）的推理时扩展方法，通过引入理论上严谨的奖励引导重掩码机制，在离散生成中实现高奖励样本生成和低奖励样本的高效修复，并在Sudoku和QM9等基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展是提升生成模型的有效范式，但现有的离散扩散模型在需要满足结构约束或优化下游奖励的生成任务中表现不足，需要更高效的奖励引导采样方法。

Method: MDM-VGB将Jerrum-Sinclair回溯马尔可夫链从固定前缀树扩展到掩码状态图，允许token在任意位置进行解掩码和重掩码，并利用奖励引导的转移偏好生成高价值部分配置。

Result: 理论证明MDM-VGB对过程验证器噪声具有鲁棒性且时间复杂度为二次，而best-of-N等启发式方法因误差累积可能指数复杂度；在Sudoku和QM9等约束满足和科学基准上实证表现优异。

Conclusion: MDM-VGB为离散扩散模型提供了理论严谨的奖励引导推理时扩展方法，兼具鲁棒性和高效性，在结构约束生成任务中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling is a promising paradigm to improve generative models, especially when outputs must satisfy structural constraints or optimize downstream rewards. We consider Masked Diffusion Model (MDM) and introduce MDM-VGB, a discrete diffusion sampler that augments unmasking generation with theoretically principled reward-guided remasking. Inspired by the recent success of the classical Jerrum-Sinclair backtracking Markov chain in reward-tilted generation, MDM-VGB extends the backtracking random walk from a fixed prefix tree to a masked-state graph, allowing tokens to be unmasked and remasked at arbitrary positions. The resulting sampler favors unmasking and remasking moves that lead to higher-value partial configurations, enabling both effective high-reward generation and efficient repair of low-reward samples. We prove that MDM-VGB is robust to process-verifier noise and achieves quadratic complexity, while popular test-time heuristics such as best-of-$N$ can incur exponential complexity due to error accumulation. Our theoretical findings are corroborated by strong empirical performance, particularly on popular constraint-satisfaction and scientific benchmarks such as Sudoku and QM9.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文借鉴互联网发展理念，提出了世界AI模型网络（AI-ModelNet）的概念、愿景和系统架构，旨在通过建立模型间的通路实现互联互通、能力共享和协同推理，解决大模型应用中高成本和部署复杂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型训练成本高、部署复杂，且异构模型广泛分布但缺乏有效的交互协作机制，这成为大模型发展的关键瓶颈。受互联网发展启发，需要一种新型范式实现模型间的互联与协作。

Method: 提出AI-ModelNet的概念与分层系统架构，通过建立模型间的通路实现互联与协作；回顾单模型与多模型研究现状；构建原型系统并通过多样化应用案例验证可行性。

Result: 通过原型系统和多样化应用案例验证了AI-ModelNet框架的可行性。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet是一种实现模型间互联互通、能力共享和协同推理的新范式，初步验证了其可行性，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [118] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究了人格提示（agreeableness高低）对多智能体LLM团队在三个任务领域（结构化编程、开放式研究协作、竞争性谈判）中的表现影响，发现人格效应高度依赖于任务结构：编程任务中低宜人性仅改变沟通风格而不影响里程碑完成，但在开放式协作和谈判中则显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已表明人格提示能改变LLM的沟通风格（如低宜人性产生对抗性语言，高宜人性产生合作性语言），但沟通风格的变化是否会客观影响任务绩效尚未在多领域得到系统检验。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过操纵前沿LLM的宜人性人格特质，在三个任务领域（结构化编程、开放式研究协作、竞争性谈判）上比较多智能体团队的表现差异。

Result: 人格效应取决于任务结构：编程任务中低宜人性引起较大沟通风格转变但对里程碑完成影响微小；开放式协作和谈判中相同操纵显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 人格操纵对多智能体系统性能的影响具有任务依赖性，为多智能体系统设计提供了启示，同时指出了人格操纵的局限性。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [119] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段训练范式（WM-AMT、FE-SFT、FC-RL），通过在自回归模型中内化前瞻性规划能力（状态推演+计划条件成功估计），弥合格式与能力之间的差距，使LLM智能体在长程任务中具备基于世界模型的未来感知决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM智能体在长程任务中本质上是反应式的，缺乏像人类一样的"what-if"推理能力（即利用内部世界模型模拟未来结果来评估潜在计划）。作者发现仅对前瞻性轨迹进行微调会导致表面模仿而非真正的预测性推理，存在"格式-能力差距"。

Method: 提出三阶段训练范式：(i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT)——向策略注入潜在预测能力；(ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT)——结构化组织已注入的能力；(iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL)——优化生成模拟的校准度和实用性。

Result: 在搜索和数学推理任务上，该方法持续优于其他训练基线。

Conclusion: 有效的内部世界建模需要"能力优先"的训练管线，才能实现扎实且校准的前瞻性能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [120] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为ODYSSEY的范畴论框架，用于构建可验证、保持局部真理的基础模型。该框架将基础模型组合为"foundries"（铸造厂）——即指定局部上下文覆盖、局部表示族、限制映射、粘合规则、阻碍策略、更新义务和人机交互视图的架构组件。每个foundry是一个携带论证组件的知识层。通用铸造学习（UFL）通过左Kan扩展（将局部工件滚动为候选foundry）和右Kan扩展（强制实施提升所需的限制、粘合、阻碍和论证条件）的形式化组合来构建foundry。Foundry SQL（FSQL）是一个小型类型化查询接口，使用TICKET（基于因果Kan扩展变换器的拓扑斯集成）认证来将外部或预训练模型纳入持久化ODYSSEY状态。该系统已在多种具体foundry上实现并测试，展示了相同的范畴论机制支持领域构建、工件回放、层诊断、基于Toulmin论证/局部LLM的审查、残余阻碍分类账以及跨异构源的优化TICKET兼容因果声明提取。本文将在ICML 2026以2.5小时教程形式呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础模型缺乏形式化的可验证性与局部真理保持机制，难以在复杂异构知识源上构建可信赖的模型组合。需要一种范畴论驱动的框架，使模型组件在局部语义约束下可组合、可验证、可审计。

Method: 采用范畴论中的层（sheaf）理论、Kan扩展和拓扑斯（topos）作为核心数学工具。具体方法包括：(1) foundry作为知识层架构，包含覆盖、表示族、限制映射等组件；(2) 左/右Kan扩展分别负责候选foundry的构建和约束条件的强制实施；(3) TICKET认证机制确保外部模型兼容性；(4) Foundry SQL提供类型化查询接口。

Result: 实现并测试了多种具体foundry，包括证据/论证、操作决策、机构/金融、市场意义、科学挑战、研究项目、助手构建和评估工具等类型。实验验证了领域构建、工件回放、层诊断、Toulmin论证审查、残余阻碍分类账和因果声明提取等功能的有效性。

Conclusion: ODYSSEY框架通过范畴论的形式化机制为构建可验证、局部真理保持的基础模型提供了统一的理论基础和实践工具，证明同一个范畴论框架能够支撑多种异构知识领域的可信模型组合与审计。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [121] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DysLexLens，一个低资源LLM框架，通过分析Reddit论坛讨论来研究失读症学习者使用AI工具的体验。该框架采用词典驱动过滤、知识图谱推理、定量评估和定性验证，并通过30个问题展示了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失读症学习者越来越多地使用AI工具辅助学习，但其真实使用体验缺乏系统研究。现有方法难以从低资源论坛数据中有效提取和分析相关经验。

Method: DysLexLens框架包含四个核心模块：1) 词典驱动过滤构建聚焦Reddit语料库；2) LLM辅助语义分析结合知识图谱查询推理；3) RAGAS和查询鲁棒性定量评估指标；4) 结构化定性验证指南（聚焦幻觉和证据对齐）。整体采用端到端、可追溯证据的架构。

Result: 使用失读症相关Reddit论坛数据和30个问题验证了DysLexLens的有效性，并展示了其对其他低资源论坛数据场景的潜在泛化能力。

Conclusion: DysLexLens为分析失读症学习者AI使用体验提供了可行方案，具有可复现性和泛化潜力。代码、样本数据、问题和评估结果已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [122] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现显式推理并不一定能提升多模态情感识别的准确性，但能增强可解释性。快速思维（直接回答）比慢速思维（深思熟虑后回答）更有效。基于此，提出MER-R1强化学习框架，通过双目标解耦和快慢置信度校准，联合优化召回率和精确率，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究显式推理在多模态情感识别中是否真正有益，以及如何调和快速直觉思维与慢速推理思维之间的矛盾，使推理真正服务于情感识别性能提升。

Method: 提出了MER-R1强化学习框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 双目标解耦——将召回率和精确率分离为两个优化信号，实现联合优化而非权衡；(2) 快慢置信度校准——将最终的慢速思维回答与快速思维直觉对齐，强化正确情感、抑制错误情感。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion数据集上达到SOTA性能，证明该框架能使推理真正有益于情感识别。

Conclusion: 快速思维（直接回答）在召回率上更优且预测更广泛自信，慢速思维（推理后回答）在精确率上更优通过保守过滤错误类别。MER-R1通过强化学习框架将两者互补性转化为显式优化，实现了性能提升并提供理论保证。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [123] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tree of Evidence (ToE)框架，一种基于分层证据推理的自动化事实检验方法，通过强化学习驱动的多源检索、证据评估和论证树聚合，在多个数据集上比基线提升4-24个百分点，尤其对抗性投毒输入效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假新闻快速传播威胁信息生态系统，尤其是GEO投毒下AI生成的错误信息可被检索系统系统性推送，污染大语言模型的推理过程，亟需可解释的自动化事实检验框架。

Method: 提出Tree of Evidence (ToE)框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 强化学习驱动的多源检索智能体；2) 证据评估智能体；3) 论证树聚合算法。通过迭代分解、检索和验证主张，构建动态扩展的论证树，形成可解释的证据链。同时提供检索过程的理论分析，推导形式化误差界。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干大语言模型上，ToE相比竞争基线提升4-24个百分点，在对抗性投毒输入上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: ToE通过层次化证据推理和强化学习策略，有效提升自动化事实检验的准确性和鲁棒性，且理论证明学习策略能收敛到信息论最优策略的邻域。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [124] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出符号反馈驱动的迭代自精炼框架，通过自然语言提示映射逻辑符号、符号验证器识别错误并转化为修正指令、计划识别器推断目标可达性，提升LLM在长时域规划任务中的鲁棒性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时域决策任务中常产生不可行或错误方案，其规划能力面临鲁棒性和可靠性挑战，亟需提升。

Method: (1) 自然语言提示机制将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述；(2) 符号验证器识别错误并转化为LLM可理解的修正指令；(3) 计划识别器推断目标可达性以引导朝向目标。

Result: 所提框架在长时域规划任务中一致性地提升了方案的可行性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效增强基于LLM的规划可靠性，有助于构建更可信的AI系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [125] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究图世界模型（GWM）中的长程 rollout 误差，提出统一框架并开发误差感知 GWM（结合谱正则化、rollout 一致性和关键节点加权），在合成拓扑和异质图测试中证明其能防止长程发散并保持预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多规划环境本质是图结构（智能体、工具、技能、路径和依赖关系），而非向量或图像。在这些图世界模型中，局部预测误差可能通过图结构扩散，且边是动态预测而非固定时，失败模式会变化。需要理解并控制长程 rollout 误差。

Method: 1) 提出统一固定边和动态边的 GWM 框架，引入动作节点支持节点/边/图级决策。2) 推导图值 rollout 界限，分离拓扑放大和模型放大。3) 提出联合节点-边算子用于动态边 rollout。4) 设计 Error-Aware GWM，包含谱正则化、rollout 一致性和关键节点加权。

Result: 在合成拓扑和异质智能体图测试中：rollout 误差和规划遗憾随 horizon 增长；结构演化时动态边训练不可或缺；Error-Aware GWM 能防止长程发散同时保持预测精度。真实图基准明确了 GWM 的适用范围：动态图 rollout 和智能体规划最有效，静态或稀疏预测任务仍适用专用图模型。

Conclusion: 图世界模型中的 rollout 误差会因拓扑结构放大，动态边训练对演化结构至关重要，Error-Aware GWM 有效缓解长程发散问题，且不同图任务需选择合适模型。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [126] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了基于智能体的世界模型（灵活但易出现幻觉状态变化）和参数化世界模型（可训练但规划能力较弱），并提出Grounded Iterative Language Planning (GILP)方法，通过训练轻量参数化骨干网络并与LLM推理结合，利用一致性门控机制减少幻觉，在真实GPT-4o-mini调用中将幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言智能体世界模型存在两类范式：基于智能体的模型灵活但难以用回归损失衡量其幻觉错误；参数化模型易于评估但规划能力弱。作者希望结合二者优势，在保持可训练性的同时减少幻觉状态变化。

Method: 提出GILP（Grounded Iterative Language Planning）框架：训练一个小型参数化骨干网络（提供有效动作、状态增量预测、风险和价值评估），与API-based LLM代理推理结合。LLM生成动作和想象的状态增量后，通过一致性门控机制检查两者是否一致，不一致时要求修订。

Result: 在真实GPT-4o-mini调用中，幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035；在校准模拟器消融实验中，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的额外LLM调用。

Conclusion: GILP成功融合了参数化世界模型的可训练性和基于智能体世界模型的灵活性，以较小的额外计算成本显著提升了规划成功率和鲁棒性，减少了幻觉状态变化。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [127] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATOD（退火式回合感知在线蒸馏）混合算法，结合在线蒸馏（OPD）和强化学习（RL）的互补优势，通过退火调度和回合级重加权机制，在长周期交互任务中同时实现快速模仿学习和奖励驱动优化，在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，OPD早期学习快但性能上限受限于教师模型，RL能突破上限但早期训练效率低。作者旨在结合两者的互补优势，同时实现快速模仿和更高的性能天花板。

Method: (1) 退火式OPD-RL调度：早期以OPD为主快速接近教师行为，逐步增强RL以驱动基于奖励的探索；(2) 回合级分歧-不确定性重加权（T-DUR）：软性地放大高效用回合，改善长轨迹中的密集监督信号。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上，ATOD在三种学生模型规模下平均成功率达3.03%优于OPD，23.62%优于GRPO，并超越对应的教师模型2.16%。

Conclusion: 混合在线蒸馏与强化学习的退火调度策略能有效结合二者的互补优势，实现超越教师的性能，为长周期交互任务的小型语言模型智能体训练提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [128] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NormAct基准，评估多模态大语言模型在具身自我中心环境中遵循隐性社会规范的能力。实验发现模型在显式目标达成（67.3%）与隐性规范遵从（26.4%）之间存在显著差距，并提出NormPerceptor方法将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多聚焦于显式目标达成或直接规范知识，忽略了具身规划器在行动序列中推断并应用隐性社会规范的能力，需要建立专门基准来评估和提升模型在这方面的表现。

Method: 构建NormAct基准，将隐性规范嵌入普通任务中；提出NormPerceptor，一种上下文条件化的线索生成器，在规划前推断场景相关规范。

Result: SOTA MLLMs在显式目标上达成67.3%，但隐性规范遵从仅26.4%；线索条件实验表明差距源于规范激活与情境化困难；NormPerceptor将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。

Conclusion: 具身智能体需要主动检测隐性规范、将其基于视觉证据情境化，并作为动作规划约束加以整合，这对实现社交恰当的自主行为至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [129] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SD-GPS 提出了一种以符号求解器为核心的几何问题解决框架，通过求解器驱动的自动形式化和验证性定理提出两个模块，将多模态感知与符号执行闭环结合，在 Geometry3K 和 PGPS9K 上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经符号几何推理框架存在两大瓶颈：一是自动形式化将多模态翻译视为静态任务，未考虑下游求解器的兼容性；二是定理预测阶段因固定规则库常导致演绎停滞。现有方法未能将符号求解器作为核心执行反馈信号来指导形式化与推理过程。

Method: 提出 SD-GPS 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 求解器驱动的自动形式化——基于 QwenVL3-2B，融合监督式形式语言适应与可解性引导的强化学习，以可执行性为核心训练信号；(2) 验证性定理提出——引入困境感知智能体，从当前证明状态中提出局部辅助引理，并通过符号验证过滤所有提议以保证合理性。

Result: 在 Geometry3K 和 PGPS9K 基准上，SD-GPS 在标准补全、多项选择和跨模态参考等评估设置下，一致优于现有的多模态大语言模型、纯神经方法和神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 闭合多模态感知与符号执行之间的反馈回路能显著提升几何推理能力，为神经代理如何通过形式系统实现可验证的问题求解提供了深刻见解。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor. However, current frameworks suffer from severe bottlenecks in two core stages: autoformalization, which treats multimodal translation as a static task decoupled from downstream solver compatibility, and theorem prediction, where solvers frequently hit a deductive impasse due to fixed rule libraries. To address these, we propose SD-GPS, a solver-driven framework that treats the symbolic solver as an execution oracle throughout both formalization and deduction. First, Solver-Driven Autoformalization unifies supervised formal-language adaptation and solvability-guided reinforcement learning into a single module built on QwenVL3-2B, making executability the central training signal. Second, Verified Theorem Proposing introduces an impasse-aware agent that proposes local auxiliary lemmas from current proof states, ensuring soundness by filtering all proposals through symbolic verification. Empirical evaluations on Geometry3K and PGPS9K demonstrate that SD-GPS consistently outperforms existing MLLM, neural, and neuro-symbolic methods across standard completion, multiple-choice, and cross-modal reference regimes, proving that closing the loop between multimodal perception and symbolic execution significantly improves geometric reasoning, offering profound insights into how neural agents can be grounded by formal systems to achieve verifiable problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [130] [RelBall: Relation Ball with Quaternion Rotation for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27967)
*Yike Liu,Peijia Xie,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RelBall模型，通过模变换建模语义层次、尾实体关系球建模多种基数关系，扩展Rotate3D以覆盖所有关系模式，在链接预测任务上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC模型如RotatE无法处理非交换组合模式，Rotate3D虽引入非交换性但无法建模语义层次结构，且两者均不能有效处理一对多关系。克服这些局限是本文的核心动机。

Method: RelBall在Rotate3D基础上引入两项创新：(1) 模变换，通过将抽象概念映射到小模数、具体实例映射到大模数来建模层次结构；(2) 尾实体关系球，建模一对一、一对多、多对一和多对多关系。

Result: 在多个数据集上的链接预测实验表明，RelBall相较于各类基线模型具有竞争性表现。

Conclusion: RelBall能够覆盖对称、反对称、逆、交换/非交换组合及语义层次等所有关系模式，提供可解释的层次化表示，并支持多种基数关系。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge graphs are often incomplete, lacking many valid facts. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to predict missing links using known triples, thereby enhancing graph coverage. A key challenge is modeling diverse relational patterns such as symmetry, antisymmetry, inversion, composition and semantic hierarchy. Existing models such as RotatE can capture symmetric, antisymmetric, inverse, and commutative composition patterns, yet struggle with non-commutative composition. Rotate3D addresses this by introducing non-commutativity via three-dimensional rotations, but still fails to capture the semantic hierarchies prevalent in knowledge graphs. Moreover, both models cannot effectively model one-to-many relations. To overcome these limitations, we propose RelBall, which extends Rotate3D with two innovations. First, our model introduces modulus transformation to model hierarchies, driving abstract concepts toward smaller moduli and concrete instances toward larger ones. Second, it introduces a tail-centric relation ball to model one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. RelBall offers the following advantages: (1) coverage of all relational patterns, including the ones mentioned above; (2) an interpretable hierarchical representation where the modulus directly reflect semantic levels; (3) support for one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate RelBall's competitive link prediction performance against various baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Lifted Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28024)
*Malte Luttermann,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化因果因子图（PCFG）及部分有向PCFG（PD-PCFG），在关系域中实现因果效应的提升推理（Lifted Causal Inference, LCI），大幅加速因果推断速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断在命题层面（如因果贝叶斯网络）计算开销大，难以处理大规模关系域；已有提升推理技术可加速概率查询，但尚未应用于因果推断。

Method: 1) 提出PCFG，将因果知识融入提升模型并定义干预的形式语义；2) 提出LCI算法，在提升层计算因果效应；3) 扩展PD-PCFG处理部分因果知识，并扩展LCI实现部分有向场景下的提升因果推断。

Result: LCI算法在提升层计算因果效应，相比命题级因果推断（如因果贝叶斯网络）大幅加速；PD-PCFG扩展了适用模型范围，降低了对因果关系的先验知识要求。

Conclusion: 将提升推理技术引入因果推断领域是有效的，能够在保持精确结果的同时显著提升计算效率，且通过处理部分因果知识进一步拓展了应用场景。

Abstract: Lifted inference exploits indistinguishabilities in probabilistic graphical models by using a representative for indistinguishable objects, thereby speeding up query answering while maintaining exact answers. In this article, we show how lifting can be applied to efficiently compute causal effects in relational domains. More specifically, we introduce parametric causal factor graphs (PCFGs) to incorporate causal knowledge in lifted models and give a formal semantics of interventions therein. We further present the Lifted Causal Inference (LCI) algorithm to compute causal effects on a lifted level, thereby drastically speeding up causal inference compared to propositional inference, e.g., in causal Bayesian networks. In addition, we present partially directed parametric causal factor graphs (PD-PCFGs) as a generalisation of PCFGs to handle partial causal knowledge and extend LCI to perform lifted causal inference in a PD-PCFG, thereby extending the applicability of lifted causal inference to a broader range of models requiring less prior knowledge about causal relationships.

</details>


### [132] [JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC) V1: An Industrial-Scale LLM/VLM-Centric Solution for Item Understanding, Management, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28070)
*Oxygen AIIC,Chan Long,Chao Liu,Chaofan Chen,Chaohui Dong,Chunyuan Guo,Danping Liu,Debin Liu,Deping Xiang,Fulai Xu,Guangyue Liu,Hao Li,Huichun Hu,Jian Yang,Jianan Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Jiaoyang Li,Jiaxing Wang,Jinglong Li,Jinjin Guo,Jun Fang,Jun Liu,Kai Zhou,Li Wang,Lili Gao,Liying Chen,Luning Yang,Mengdi Zhou,Pengzhang Liu,Qi Lv,Qianyun Wang,Qixia Jiang,Ruyue Li,Shimu Liang,Shuxing Wang,Sijie Zhang,Siqi Li,Tianhao Gao,Wang Ke,Weihu Huang,Wencan Lai,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaohui Zhang,Xiaojing Dong,Ya Liu,Yifeng Zhang,Yixiang Wang,Yongtai Zhang,Yongyi Liao,Zhaoru Chen,Zhen Chen,Zhiyong Ma,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhongwei Liu,Ziyan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 京东发布基于LLM/VLM的工业级商品知识生产与服务平台Oxygen AIIC，覆盖数十亿SKU，实现94.2%精度和82.8%召回的知识生产，已部署于搜索、推荐等核心业务场景并取得显著业务收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 京东拥有超7亿活跃用户、数十亿SKU的庞大规模，高质量结构化商品知识对提升用户体验、降低管理成本和提高运营效率至关重要，但面临快速涌现的新概念、海量SKU的高质量知识生产以及多样化的下游需求三大工业级挑战。

Method: Oxygen AIIC围绕四大核心支柱构建：(i) 高效人机协同驱动的本体工程，支持百万级条目的动态演化与敏捷扩展；(ii) "语义搜索后判别"(S2D)知识识别架构，结合吞吐优化策略实现数十亿SKU的可扩展、高吞吐AI商品库生产；(iii) 自演进的商品理解LLM/VLM，稳定可控地提升模型性能；(iv) 统一商品隧道作为数据与服务枢纽。

Result: 知识生产精度达94.2%，召回率达82.8%；覆盖数万京东类目，每日处理数亿次商品更新（运行于华为昇腾NPU）；累计积累数千亿商品知识资产；搜索流量覆盖率达80.4%，商品信息质量问题下降37%，商品上架核心属性自动填充率超80%。

Conclusion: Oxygen AIIC证明了基于大模型的工业级商品知识平台在超大规模电商场景下的可行性与有效性，通过本体工程、知识识别架构、自演进模型和统一服务枢纽的系统化设计，成功应对了电商知识生产的三大工业级挑战，并在搜索、推荐、运营、品类规划等核心业务中带来了可量化的规模化收益。

Abstract: JD.com, one of the world's largest e-commerce platforms, serves over 700 million active users and millions of merchants, with a catalog of tens of billions of SKUs. At this scale, high-quality, structured item knowledge underpins a better consumer experience, lower management costs, and higher operational efficiency-yet producing and serving it poses three industrial-scale challenges: fast-emerging concepts, high-quality knowledge production for massive SKUs, and diverse downstream requirements. To address these challenges, we present the JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC), an industrial-scale platform built on LLMs/VLMs for item-knowledge production and service. Oxygen AIIC is built around four core pillars: (i) ontology engineering driven by efficient human-AI collaboration, which supports the dynamic evolution and agile expansion of an ontology with millions of entries; (ii) a "Semantic Search then Discrimination"(S2D) knowledge identification architecture that, combined with throughput improvement strategies, enables scalable, extensible, and high-throughput AI Item Library production for tens of billions of SKUs; (iii) self-evolving item-understanding LLMs/VLMs that improve in a stable and controllable manner, enabling knowledge production with 94.2% precision and 82.8% recall; and (iv) a unified item tunnel that serves as the data and service hub. Oxygen AIIC now covers tens of thousands of JD categories and processes hundreds of millions of item updates per day on Huawei Ascend NPUs. It has accumulated hundreds of billions of item-knowledge assets. Deployed across core business scenarios-including search, recommendation, operations, category planning-Oxygen AIIC has delivered measurable gains at scale. Search-traffic coverage reaches 80.4%, item-information quality issues drop by 37%, the automated fill rate of core attributes during item listing exceeds 80%.

</details>


### [133] [Ontology-Guided Evidence Path Inference for Multi-hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28076)
*Yongxue Shan,Meihan Wu,Cundi Fang,Jie Peng,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OPI框架，利用本体引导的路径推理，通过关系中心的本体图、双向检索机制和迭代精炼策略，有效减少搜索空间并提升多跳KGQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于主题中心扩展的多跳KGQA方法面临两大挑战：搜索空间随噪声混合路径快速增长，以及检索路径无法满足复杂问题的语义约束。

Method: 提出OPI框架：(1) 构建关系中心的本体图，捕获关系的头尾类型约束；(2) 基于本体图的双向检索机制，通过预测答案类型映射到兼容的最终跳关系，结合主题侧前缀扩展与答案侧最终跳匹配；(3) 迭代精炼策略，在问题上下文下重新评估检索路径和候选答案，过滤类型兼容但与问题无关的证据。

Result: 在WebQSP上Hit@1/F1分别提升4.6/5.0，在CWQ上提升8.9/3.3，在MetaQA上仅用检索模块即达到近乎饱和的Hit@1。

Conclusion: OPI通过本体引导有效抑制噪声路径扩展，显著提升多跳KGQA的准确率和效率。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) aims to answer natural-language questions by reasoning over structured facts. Existing multi-hop KGQA methods mainly rely on topic-centered expansion, which faces two key challenges: the search space rapidly grows with noisy mixed-type paths, and retrieved paths may fail to satisfy the semantic constraints of complex questions. To address these challenges, we propose OPI, an ontology-guided evidence path inference framework for multi-hop KGQA. OPI introduces a relation-centric ontology graph to capture the head-tail type constraints of relations, providing a compact interface for answer-side constraints. Based on this ontology graph, OPI first introduces a bidirectional retrieval mechanism by mapping the predicted answer type to compatible final-hop relations and combining topic-side prefix expansion with answer-side final-hop matching, thereby suppressing noisy mixed-type expansion. OPI further adopts an iterative refinement strategy to reassess retrieved paths and candidate answers under the question context, filtering type-compatible but question-irrelevant evidence for more reliable answer prediction. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and MetaQA show that OPI substantially reduces the search space, improves Hit@1/F1 by 4.6/5.0 points on WebQSP and 8.9/3.3 points on CWQ over the strongest prior results, and achieves near-saturated Hit@1 on MetaQA with the retrieval module alone.

</details>


### [134] [AI-Driven Synthesis for High-Tech System Design: Automating Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28126)
*Luuk Oerlemans,Steven Westerhof,Theo Hofman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自动化设计（AiD）范式，利用深度学习与生成式AI的计算设计综合（CDS）框架，通过两个案例（电驱动系统设计与空间尺寸问题）验证了从仿真优化向自主设计转变的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高科技系统设计中存在固有的组合复杂性，传统设计方法难以应对，亟需一种能够自动化生成新颖系统的新范式来减少对人类监督的依赖。

Method: 提出了计算设计综合（CDS）框架，该框架结合深度学习与生成式AI技术，用于自动化系统设计；并通过电驱动系统设计和空间尺寸问题两个案例进行验证。

Result: 两个案例研究证明了该方法作为概念验证点的有效性，展示了AI驱动方法能够实现从仿真优化到自主设计的根本性转变。

Conclusion: 自动化设计（AiD）作为一种变革性范式，能够有效应对高科技系统设计的组合复杂性，推动工程领域向最少人工监督的自主设计演进。

Abstract: This article addresses the combinatorial complexity inherent in modern high-tech system design by presenting automation-in-design (AiD) as a transformative paradigm. We propose computational design synthesis (CDS), a framework utilising deep learning and generative AI to automate the creation of novel systems. Two case studies (e-drive system design and spatial dimensioning problem) serve as proof-points for this approach. The AI-driven methods used in the case studies represent a fundamental shift in engineering, advancing from simulation-based optimisation towards autonomous design with minimal human supervision.

</details>


### [135] [Tandem Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28166)
*Difan Jiao,Raghav Singhal,Robert West,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL)，将"双人训练"范式引入基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)中。TRL让一个较强的"高级模型"和一个冻结的"初级模型"交替协作生成推理链，共同获得奖励。在竞赛数学任务上训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct，结果表明TRL在保持与标准GRPO相当的推理能力的同时，提升了与初级模型的交接鲁棒性、减少了分布漂移，并使思维链更易于初级模型理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RLVR显著提升了大模型的推理能力，但模型推理逐渐走向人类难以理解、可读性差、语言混杂等特异模式，导致弱智能体或人类难以有效利用其推理能力。现有双人训练范式仅在概念验证层面被展示，尚未验证其能否扩展到现代RLVR的长思维链场景。

Method: TRL的核心方法：(1) 高级模型与一个冻结的初级模型随机交替协作生成推理链；(2) 对协作生成的完整推理进行奖励打分；(3) 仅对高级模型应用标准GRPO损失进行训练。这样高级模型被驱动以初级模型能够跟随的方式进行推理。

Result: 在竞赛数学任务上训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct，TRL在单人推理能力上与标准GRPO持平，同时从相同的协作生成结构中涌现出三种特性：与初级模型更强的交接鲁棒性、更少的分布漂移、以及更易于初级模型理解的思维链。

Conclusion: TRL展示了RLVR中提升多模型通信和人类兼容性的可行路径，在保持模型自身推理能力的同时显著改善了推理过程的可理解性和协作性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capability of large language models, reaching expert or even superhuman performance in domains such as competition math. However, whether weaker agents and humans can actually harness this capability is far less certain, with RLVR documented to drift reasoning toward idiosyncratic patterns such as poor readability and language mixing. Tandem training is a recently introduced paradigm that targets this compatibility problem: a trained, stronger senior co-generates each rollout with a frozen, weaker junior, and the two are rewarded as a team, so the senior is pushed to reason in ways the junior can follow. Yet this paradigm has so far been demonstrated only in proof-of-concept settings, leaving open whether it scales to the long chains of thought of the modern RLVR pipeline. In this work, we propose Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), which carries the tandem training paradigm into RLVR. In TRL, the senior and a frozen junior alternate stochastically to co-generate the reasoning, the resulting generation is rewarded, and the standard GRPO loss is applied to the senior. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on competition math, we find that TRL matches vanilla GRPO on solo reasoning capability while three properties emerge together from the same rollout structure: stronger handoff robustness with the junior, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and a chain-of-thought more legible to the junior. Our results demonstrate a promising route for RLVR with practical payoffs in multi-model communication and human compatibility.

</details>


### [136] [Agent-Native Immune System: Architecture, Taxonomy, and Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28270)
*Bo Shen,Lifeng Chang,Tianyuan Wei,Yunpeng Li,Feng Shi,Yichen Han,Peijie Gao,Shiyi Kuang,Xin Chang,Dehui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS)的生物启发式内生防御架构，将其直接嵌入智能体的认知循环中，以解决当前AI系统在运行时面临的内存投毒、工具链操纵和多智能体协议攻击等安全威胁。ANIS通过六层免疫塔（L0-L5）、统一的Agent病毒/疫苗分类体系、三重监控骨干（Meta, Self, Auto）以及持续免疫学习机制，实现了动态运行时防护，并严格区分了模型对齐（静态宪法基础）与智能体免疫（动态执法机制）的理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从静态聊天机器人向具备持久记忆、工具调用和多智能体协作的自主智能体转变，极大地扩展了AI威胁面。现有外围安全和训练时对齐等防御机制位于智能体主动推理循环之外，无法有效防御运行时劫持攻击（如内存投毒、工具链操纵、多智能体协议攻击），存在关键安全空白。

Method: 提出了Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS)，一种生物启发的内生防御架构，包含四个核心贡献：1）六层免疫塔（L0-L5），其中L1屏障免疫作为非认知的物理与逻辑隔离层；2）统一的Agent病毒与Agent疫苗分类体系，区分浅层非参数防御与稳健参数疫苗；3）Harness Triad（Meta, Self, Auto）自监控元认知自动化骨干，驱动持续免疫学习（CIL）实现疫苗动态适应新威胁；4）严格区分模型对齐（静态）与智能体免疫（动态运行时机制）的理论边界。

Result: 论文给出了ANIS框架的完整理论设计，包括六层免疫架构、病毒/疫苗分类体系、持续免疫学习机制以及免疫与对齐的理论区分。同时提出了开放挑战：免疫协议标准化、新评估指标（如自身免疫率，即假阳性干预率）、以及病原体与疫苗在集体智能生态系统中的共同演化动力学。

Conclusion: ANIS作为首个内生式、生物启发的智能体原生防御架构，将安全机制直接嵌入智能体的认知循环中，弥补了现有外挂式防御的运行时安全空白。通过免疫塔、病毒/疫苗分类学、持续免疫学习和理论框架的建立，为自主智能体的运行时安全提供了全新范式，并为后续研究指明了标准化、评估指标和共同演化动力学等关键方向。

Abstract: The transition from static chat bots to autonomous agents--equipped with persistent memory, tool-use protocols, and multi-agent collaboration--has fundamentally expanded the AI threat landscape. Current defense mechanisms, such as perimeter security and training-time alignment, remain external to the agent's active reasoning loop. Consequently, they fall short: a fully aligned agent remains highly vulnerable to runtime hijacking via memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, or multi-agent protocol attacks. To address this critical gap, we introduce the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), the first biologically inspired, endogenous defense architecture embedded directly within the agent's cognitive loop. Our framework presents four primary contributions. First, we design a six-layer Immune Tower (L0-L5), distinctly incorporating Barrier Immunity (L1) as a non-cognitive, physical-and-logical isolation layer. Second, we establish a unified taxonomy of Agent Viruses and Agent Vaccines, formalizing the critical distinction between superficial non-parametric defenses and robust parametric vaccines. Third, we conceptualize the Harness Triad--Meta, Self, and Auto--a self-monitoring, meta-cognitive automation backbone that drives Continual Immune Learning (CIL), enabling vaccines to dynamically adapt to novel threats. Finally, we establish a rigorous theoretical demarcation between model alignment and agent immunity: while alignment provides a static "constitutional" value foundation during training, ANIS serves as the dynamic "law enforcement" mechanism during runtime. We conclude by framing open challenges for the field, including immune protocol standardization, novel evaluation metrics such as the Autoimmunity Rate (false-positive intervention rate), and the co-evolutionary dynamics between pathogens and vaccines within collective intelligence ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [On strongly quasiconvex pseudomonotone equilibrium problems in Hadamard spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27563)
*Luisa Marie Després,Nicholas Pischke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Hadamard空间（非正曲率完备测地度量空间）中用于寻找伪单调且强拟凸双函数均衡点的两种邻近点型方法。证明了两方法的强收敛性，并给出次线性非渐近收敛率，放松了Iusem和Lara先前工作中的若干假设。同时证明了在Hadamard空间上适当半连续、伪单调且强拟凸双函数生成的均衡问题解的存在性，并由此推出每个下半连续强拟凸函数在Hadamard空间上均有极小元，回答了第二作者的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Iusem和Lara在欧氏空间中关于伪单调强拟凸双函数均衡点的邻近点方法到一般的Hadamard空间，并改进其理论结果（减弱假设、获得定量收敛保证）。

Method: 采用两种邻近点型迭代方法，在Hadamard空间中利用测地度量的几何性质，以初等方式分析伪单调且强拟凸双函数的均衡点问题。论证简洁且有效，可导出次线性非渐近收敛速率。

Result: 证明了两种方法在一般Hadamard空间上的强收敛性，给出了次线性非渐近收敛保证（这在欧氏空间中也是新结果）。放松或完全去除了Iusem和Lara工作中的多项假设。证明了在Hadamard空间上适当半连续、伪单调且强拟凸双函数生成的均衡问题解的存在性，并推出每个下半连续强拟凸函数均有极小元。

Conclusion: 本文的方法和结果显著推广了现有理论，在更一般的空间框架下以更弱的条件获得了更强的收敛结论（含定量估计），并解答了关于Hadamard空间上强拟凸函数极小元存在的开放问题。

Abstract: We study two proximal point type methods for finding equilibrium points of pseudomonotone and strongly quasiconvex bifunctions. Extending results by A. Iusem and F. Lara, we prove the strong convergence of these methods over general complete geodesic metric spaces of nonpositive curvature, so-called Hadamard spaces. Our arguments are quite elementary and in particular effective, yielding sublinear non-asymptotic guarantees for the distance of the iterates towards the solution. These quantitative results are novel even in the context of Euclidean spaces, the original setting of the work by Iusem and Lara, and the simplicity of our arguments allows us to either weaken or even fully discharge various assumptions featuring in this previous work. We also provide an existence result for solutions of equilibrium problems generated by suitably semicontinuous, pseudomonotone and strongly quasiconvex bifunctions over general Hadamard spaces, and derive from this that every lower-semicontinuous strongly quasiconvex function over a Hadamard space has a minimizer, answering a question of the second author.

</details>


### [138] [Uncertainty-aware Power System Planning via Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27602)
*Mehrnoush Ghazanfariharandi,Robert Mieth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电力系统扩展规划的计算方法，显式考虑两阶段日前（DA）和实时（RT）运行决策中的不确定性，同时保留时间耦合约束以建模发电机爬坡和储能。采用投影随机梯度下降算法结合原始-对偶优化框架及指数移动平均平滑策略求解，实验表明该方法相比传统完美知识模型能降低总系统成本并实现可再生能源整合目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电力系统规划模型为保持计算可处理性，通常将多阶段运行过程简化为单阶段，忽略了各运行阶段的不确定性。随着负荷模式变化和依赖天气的随机可再生能源普及，这种不确定性对运行的影响日益显著，忽略它已被证明会导致输电容量和灵活资源投资不足。

Method: 提出一种显式考虑两阶段日前和实时运行决策不确定性的扩展规划方法，保留时间耦合约束；采用投影随机梯度下降算法结合原始-对偶优化框架，并引入指数移动平均平滑策略提高收敛稳定性。

Result: 在两阶段日前和实时仿真框架中评估投资决策，与传统假设完美可再生能源知识的扩展规划模型对比，所提出框架实现了更低的总系统成本，同时确保技术组合达到设定的可再生能源整合目标。

Conclusion: 显式建模多阶段运行不确定性有助于制定更经济有效的投资策略，避免因忽略不确定性导致的投资不足问题，对高比例可再生能源电力系统规划具有重要意义。

Abstract: Power system planning models provide important guidance on long-term investment strategies with significant socio-economic impact. To remain computationally manageable, however, such planning models compromise on the level of complexity with which power system operations and physics are captured. A common approach in most planning models is to collapse multi-stage power system operational processes into a single stage and, as a result, give up on the ability to account for uncertainty in each operational stage. In light of newly emerging load patterns and the continuing adoption of weather-dependent stochastic renewable generation, this uncertainty, however, becomes increasingly impactful on operations, and ignoring it has been shown to cause underinvestment in transmission capacity and flexible resources. In this work, we present a computational approach for power system expansion planning that explicitly considers two-stage day-ahead (DA) and real-time (RT) operational decisions under uncertainty while retaining time-coupling constraints to allow modeling generator ramping and energy storage. To solve the resulting optimization problem efficiently, we employ a projected stochastic gradient descent algorithm combined with a primal-dual optimization framework and an exponential moving average smoothing strategy to improve convergence stability. We evaluate the resulting investment decisions within a two-stage DA and RT simulation framework and compare them with a classic expansion planning model that assumes perfect knowledge of renewable generation. Our experiments show that the proposed framework achieves lower total system costs while ensuring that the implemented technology portfolio achieves set renewable integration targets.

</details>


### [139] [Policy Gradient Learning for Distributionally Robust Markov Decision Processes under Wasserstein Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27610)
*Yadh Hafsi,Samy Mekkaoui,Huyên Pham,Kaixin Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对转移核存在分布不确定性的有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，提出了Wasserstein分布鲁棒控制策略梯度框架，通过Wasserstein对偶重构和方向可微性分析得到鲁棒策略梯度的显式递推表征，并基于此设计了鲁棒演员-评论员算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的MDP转移核往往存在分布不确定性（如模型估计误差、环境扰动），而传统策略梯度方法无法直接处理这种不确定性，因为最坏情况转移律隐式依赖于策略参数，导致标准策略梯度不适用。

Method: 1) 使用状态-动作依赖的Wasserstein球刻画转移核的不确定性；2) 建立max-min鲁棒控制问题；3) 通过Wasserstein对偶重构鲁棒Bellman递归；4) 分析鲁棒Bellman递归的方向可微性，得到鲁棒策略梯度的显式递推表征；5) 基于该表征设计鲁棒演员-评论员算法。

Result: 在离散和连续基准示例上验证了所提鲁棒演员-评论员算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将策略梯度方法推广到Wasserstein分布鲁棒MDP，解决了因最坏情况转移律隐式依赖策略参数导致的标准策略梯度失效问题，为鲁棒强化学习提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) under distributional uncertainty in the transition kernels and develop a policy-gradient framework for Wasserstein distributionally robust control. Ambiguity is modeled by state-action dependent Wasserstein balls around nominal transition kernels, leading to a max-min control problem over randomized policies and admissible transition laws. Since the worst-case transition law depends implicitly on the policy parameters, the usual policy-gradient argument does not apply. We address this difficulty by using a Wasserstein dual reformulation of the robust Bellman recursion and analyzing its directional differentiability. This yields an explicit recursive characterization of the robust policy gradient. Building on this characterization, we propose a robust actor-critic algorithm and illustrate its behavior on discrete and continuous benchmark examples.

</details>


### [140] [Multidimensional quadratic BSDEs with weak interactions and their applications in mean-field games of controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27640)
*Ulrich Horst,Emil Schmidek,Huilin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维二次BSDE在价格影响下的平均场效用最大化模型中的适定性，在适当小性条件下建立了存在唯一性，并推导了向Nash和Radner均衡的定量收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维二次BSDE的适定性是BSDE理论中的核心未解问题。本文受带价格影响的平均场效用最大化模型启发，引入一类超出已有适定性结果范围的新多维qBSDE，旨在研究当参与者数量趋于无穷时的极限行为。

Method: 在适当的与维度无关的小性条件下，利用Picard迭代建立此类qBSDE的存在唯一性；进而基于稳定性结果，推导Nash均衡和Radner均衡两种设定下向相应平均场均衡的定量收敛速度。

Result: 建立了与维度无关的小性条件下多维qBSDE的存在唯一性；该条件自然回避了无小性假设时一般多维qBSDE可能无解的问题；得到了Picard迭代的稳定性结果；给出了Nash均衡和Radner均衡两种情形下向平均场均衡的定量收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的小性条件有效克服了多维qBSDE的适定性障碍，且系统本身不局限于小邻域，使得分析相应效用最大化问题的平均场极限成为可能。

Abstract: The well-posedness of multidimensional quadratic backward stochastic differential equations (qBSDEs) remains one of the central open problems in BSDE theory. Motivated by a mean-field utility maximization model with price impact, we introduce a new class of multidimensional qBSDEs that lies beyond the scope of existing well-posedness results (see (2.15) for the generator). In order to study the limit as the number of players tends to infinity, we establish existence and uniqueness for a large class of such qBSDEs under suitable smallness conditions imposed on each individual dynamics. A key feature of our approach is that the smallness condition is independent of the dimension of the BSDE system. In particular, the system itself is not confined to a small neighborhood, which allows us to analyze the mean-field limit of the underlying utility maximization problem. Such a condition is also natural in view of the well-known fact that general multidimensional qBSDEs may fail to admit solutions in the absence of suitable smallness assumptions. In addition, we derive a stability result for this class of equations based on the application of Picard iterations. Finally, using this stability result, we establish quantitative convergence rates toward the corresponding mean-field equilibria in two settings: Nash and Radner equilibria.

</details>


### [141] [A Backstepping Framework for Unconstrained Accelerated Optimization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27722)
*Song Chen,Jiaxu Liu,Chao Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反步法（backstepping）的控制理论视角来研究无约束优化算法。通过将优化过程建模为增广严格反馈系统，利用反步法递归合成反馈律，统一了常参数Nesterov流和PID加速优化器等经典方法，并证明了控制律的最优性条件依赖于虚拟控制所定义的目标流形，最终建立了最优反步定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将无约束优化算法的发展重新表述为反馈控制问题，利用控制理论中的反步法为优化算法的分析与设计提供统一且鲁棒的框架。

Method: 将优化过程建模为增广严格反馈系统 $\dot{x}_1 = x_2, \dot{x}_2 = u, \dot{z} = q(x_1,z)$，输出为 $y = \nabla f(x_1)$。通过反步法递归合成虚拟控制与实际反馈律 $u$；对于凸目标函数，分别处理增广和标准严格反馈情形；建立逆最优性分析与最优反步定理。

Result: 成功恢复了常参数Nesterov流和PID加速优化器作为直接推论；证明了在固定虚拟控制下，通用第二步律关于诱导的外跟踪问题是逆最优的；揭示了控制律的最优性条件性地依赖于虚拟控制所规定的目标流形。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个基于反步法的通用范式，用于连续时间无约束优化算法的分析与设计，具有鲁棒性和普适性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a control-theoretic perspective on unconstrained optimization algorithms using the backstepping methods. We model the optimization process as an augmented strict-feedback system given by $\dot{x}_1 = x_2$, $\dot{x}_2 = u$, and $\dot{z} = q(x_1,z)$, with a regulated output $y = \nabla f(x_1)$. This formulation recasts the development of unconstrained optimization algorithms as a feedback control problem, where the goal is to design the input $u$ to ensure $y(t) \to 0$. By employing backstepping, we recursively synthesize the actual feedback law $u$ after initially selecting a virtual control for $x_1$. For convex objective functions, we develop a general synthesis framework for augmented strict-feedback systems and specialize it to the standard strict-feedback case. This unified framework successfully recovers the constant-parameter Nesterov flow and the proportional-integral-derivative (PID) accelerated optimizer as direct corollaries. We further establish that, given a fixed virtual control, the universal second-step law is inverse optimal with respect to an induced outer-tracking problem. This reveals that the optimality of the control law is conditionally dependent on the target manifold prescribed by the virtual control, rather than holding globally across all possible backstepping designs. Finally, we formulate a formal optimal-backstepping theorem that elevates this optimality principle to the virtual-control stage by solving a reduced Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman problem. These contributions collectively yield a robust and general backstepping-driven paradigm for the analysis and design of continuous-time unconstrained optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [142] [A primal-dual splitting algorithm for monotone inclusions with applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28010)
*Changchi Huang,Jigen Peng,Liqian Qin,Yuchao Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的原始-对偶分裂算法，用于求解希尔伯特空间中一类广泛的单调包含问题。该算法统一并推广了多种现有方法，每轮迭代仅需一次算子求值或预解计算，并在标准假设下证明了弱收敛性，在均匀单调等正则条件下证明了强收敛性。在图像去模糊和去噪问题上的数值实验验证了算法的效率与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对结构化单调包含问题中存在的多单调算子、cocoercive项以及涉及线性映射的复合单调算子，现有算法往往需要多次算子评估或预解步骤，缺乏统一框架。本文旨在提出一个更通用、高效且收敛性更优的原始-对偶分裂算法，以统一和推广现有方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的原始-对偶分裂算法，对cocoercive分量使用前向评估，对单调算子使用后向预解步骤，并对线性组合项执行对偶更新。算法每轮迭代仅需一次预解或算子评估。

Result: 在标准单调性和cocoercivity假设下证明了迭代的弱收敛性；在均匀单调性等温和正则条件下进一步证明了强收敛性。图像去模糊和去噪的数值实验展示了算法的效率和灵活性。

Conclusion: 该算法成功统一并推广了多种现有分裂方法，收敛性理论完备，且在实际图像处理任务中表现高效，具有良好的理论价值与应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a broad class of structured monotone inclusion problems in real Hilbert spaces. We propose a novel primal-dual splitting algorithm for solving such inclusions, which accommodates multiple monotone operators and cocoercive terms, as well as a composite monotone operator involving the linear map. The algorithm combines forward evaluations for the cocoercive components with backward resolvent steps for the monotone operators and employs a dual update for the linear composition term. It generalizes and unifies several existing methods, while requiring only a single resolvent or operator evaluation per iteration. We prove weak convergence of the iterates under standard assumptions on monotonicity and cocoercivity. Furthermore, we establish strong convergence under a mild regularity condition, such as uniform monotonicity. Numerical experiments on image deblurring and denoising problems demonstrate the efficiency and flexibility of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [143] [Fractional quadratic obstructions to the local controllability of the Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28106)
*Thomas Perrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Burgers方程在零点的局部可控性，发现线性化系统不可控时，二次障碍可通过控制量的分数阶负Sobolev范数量化，首次在自然物理PDE中给出连续分数阶二次障碍谱，并优化了常源剖面情形下的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Burgers方程在零点附近的局部可控性，特别是线性化系统不可控时，二阶分析如何量化有限时间可控性的障碍。

Method: 通过二次障碍分析与Sobolev范数（负分数阶）量化，识别源剖面的结构条件，推导尖锐的非线性余项估计，并验证最优性。

Result: 建立了连续分数阶二次障碍谱（H^{-1}至H^{-5/4}），改进了常源剖面情形的Fourier模式算术条件，证明障碍在有限时间内持续存在。

Conclusion: 为理解PDE中分数阶障碍的起源提供了框架，障碍指数与源剖面的正则性及物理空间奇异性直接相关。

Abstract: We study the local controllability near zero of the Burgers equation with a scalar control and a fixed space-dependent source profile, in the case where the linearized system fails to be controllable and a second-order analysis is therefore required. We prove that quadratic obstructions to finite-time controllability can be quantified by Sobolev norms of the control with fractional negative exponents ranging over a full interval. To our knowledge, this is the first example, for a natural physical PDE, of a continuous scale of fractional quadratic obstructions, and the first such continuous scale for finite-time local controllability. Our explicit constructions shed light on the origin of fractional obstructions for partial differential equations, by relating the obstruction exponent to the regularity, and in some cases to the physical-space singularity, of the source profile.
  We identify the natural structural conditions on the source profile leading to obstructions quantified by the $H^{-1}$ and $H^{-5/4}$ norms of the control, thereby providing a general framework in which the previously studied case of a constant source profile fits naturally. In this constant-profile case, we improve existing results by identifying the arithmetic condition on the Fourier mode which ensures that a small-time obstruction actually persists in finite time.
  Finally, we derive sharp nonlinear remainder estimates adapted to the precise regularity of the source profile. These estimates make most of our obstruction results optimal with respect to the smallness assumption imposed on the control.

</details>


### [144] [Reservoir Zero-Coordinatewise Projected Subspace Search for Minimization Over Sparse Symmetric Sets in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28124)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Shima Shabani,Michael Breuss*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RZCW-PSS的算法，用于解决稀疏学习中非凸基数约束优化问题（NP-hard）。该算法结合坐标搜索、对称感知交换支持更新、随机低维子空间探索和零坐标库注入，并设计了精炼的库初始化策略和支持识别保护机制。在正则性、采样和子问题精度假设下，证明了算法几乎所有聚点都是Beck-Hallak零坐标平稳点，并条件性地证明了支持稳定后的局部线性收敛速率。数值实验表明，RZCW-PSS在鲁棒性和解质量上优于同类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏学习中的基数约束优化问题因稀疏约束的组合特性而具有NP-hard性质，现有方法（如Partial Simplex Search、Basic Feasible Search、Zero-Coordinatewise Search）在解质量和鲁棒性方面仍有提升空间，亟需更高效的算法。

Method: 提出RZCW-PSS算法，在稀疏流形上采用单纯形风格方法，整合以下关键技术：(1) 坐标搜索；(2) 对称感知的交换式支持更新；(3) 随机低维子空间探索；(4) 零坐标库注入（利用动态维护的先前可行点库构建稀疏兼容子空间搜索）；(5) 精炼库初始化策略（将稀疏投影直接嵌入均匀采样过程）；(6) 可选的完整支持稳定化保护机制（固定支持变化下降阈值）。

Result: 在正则性、采样和子问题精度假设下，证明RZCW-PSS迭代的每个完整支持聚点几乎必然是Beck-Hallak零坐标平稳点；启用保护机制且完整支持初始化时，该结论适用于所有聚点。条件性地证明了支持稳定后的局部线性收敛速率及相应的对数局部迭代复杂度。数值实验表明RZCW-PSS在鲁棒性和解质量上优于Partial Simplex Search、Basic Feasible Search和Zero-Coordinatewise Search，且计算成本具备竞争力。

Conclusion: RZCW-PSS通过创新的库初始化、子空间探索和稳定化保护机制，在稀疏学习非凸优化问题中实现了更强的鲁棒性和更优的解质量，同时保持计算效率，为处理NP-hard的基数约束问题提供了一种理论上有保障的实用算法。

Abstract: We study a class of nonconvex cardinality-constrained optimization problems arising in sparse learning. These problems are NP-hard due to the combinatorial nature of sparsity constraints. We introduce a Reservoir Zero-Coordinatewise Projected Subspace Search (RZCW-PSS) algorithm, a simplex-style method on sparse manifolds that integrates coordinatewise search, symmetry-aware swap-based support updates, randomized low-dimensional subspace exploration, and zero-coordinatewise reservoir injection. The proposed method augments classical coordinate and swap moves with sparse-compatible subspace searches constructed from a dynamically maintained reservoir of previously accepted feasible points. A key feature of the approach is a refined reservoir initialization strategy that embeds sparse projection directly into a uniform sampling procedure, preserving geometric diversity within the feasible set. The algorithm also includes an optional support-identification safeguard that enforces full-support stabilization under a fixed support-change decrease threshold. We establish that, under the stated regularity, sampling, and subproblem-accuracy assumptions, every full-support accumulation point of the RZCW-PSS iterates is Beck--Hallak zero-coordinatewise stationary almost surely; with the safeguard and full-support initialization, this conclusion applies to all accumulation points. We further prove a conditional local linear convergence rate after support stabilization and derive the corresponding logarithmic local iteration complexity. Numerical experiments on synthetic sparse learning problems demonstrate that RZCW-PSS improves robustness and solution quality while remaining computationally competitive with Partial Simplex Search, Basic Feasible Search, and Zero-Coordinatewise Search methods.

</details>


### [145] [Three-Body Earth-Moon Transfers with Different Departure/Arrival Orbital Altitudes: New Phenomenon and Diffusion Model-Augmented Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28207)
*Shuyue Fu,Wenxuan Zhang,Di Wu,Shengping Gong,Peng Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种扩散模型增强的网格搜索方法，用于高效构建地月转移轨道。该方法利用转移时间分布相对于出发相位角的不连续现象训练扩散模型，生成高质量初始猜测，在保证转移特性相当的情况下，收敛率提升47.34-56.25%，计算时间节省39.39-40.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网格搜索方法在地月转移轨道构建中收敛率低、计算效率差，且当改变出发/到达轨道高度时需要重复进行网格搜索和轨道修正，缺乏高效的方法来生成高质量初始猜测。

Method: 在地月平面圆型限制性三体问题框架下，考虑双脉冲地月转移。首先构建转移并探索解空间（出发相位角、初始-圆速度比、飞行时间），发现飞行时间分布相对于出发相位角的不连续现象，利用该现象训练扩散模型，用于增广传统网格搜索方法，生成不同出发/到达轨道高度下的高质量初始猜测。

Result: 提出的扩散模型增广网格搜索方法相较于传统网格搜索方法，收敛率提升47.34-56.25%，计算时间节省39.39-40.52%，同时保证可比的转移特性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可以有效增广传统网格搜索方法，通过利用解空间中的不连续分布特征生成高质量初始猜测，显著提升地月转移轨道构建的收敛率和计算效率。

Abstract: Construction of Earth-Moon transfers is the basis of missions to explore the Moon and cislunar space. The traditional grid search method suffers from a relatively low convergence rate and computational efficiency, mainly focusing on the distribution of transfer characteristic parameters. Moreover, when constructing transfers with different departure/arrival orbital altitudes, the process of grid search and trajectory correction should be repeated with a low convergence rate and computational efficiency. To address these limitations of the traditional grid search method, this paper is devoted to exploring an effective way to augment the grid search method. Bi-impulsive Earth-Moon transfers from a circular Earth parking orbit to a circular Moon target orbit in the Earth-Moon planar circular restricted three-body problem are considered in this paper. Firstly, the transfers are constructed, and the corresponding solution space is explored in terms of construction parameters, including departure phase angle at the Earth parking orbit, initial-to-circular velocity ratio, and time of flight. An interesting phenomenon about the discontinuous behavior of the time-of-flight distribution with respect to departure phase angle is identified. This phenomenon is further used to train a diffusion model, which aims to augment the traditional grid search method and generate high-quality initial guesses for transfers with different departure/arrival orbital altitudes. The construction results of the proposed method are presented and analyzed. The proposed diffusion model-augmented grid search method improves the convergence rate by 47.34-56.25% and saves the wall-clock time by 39.39-40.52% over the traditional grid search method relatively, while ensuring comparable transfer characteristics.

</details>


### [146] [A Fletcher's Augmented Lagrangian-Based Stochastic First-Order Method for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28230)
*Yawen Cui,Qiankun Shi,Xiao Wang,Xiantao Xiao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸等式约束优化问题，提出了一种基于分解随机搜索方向的随机一阶方法，利用Fletcher增广拉格朗日函数作为步长选择的平滑价值函数，并引入基于最小奇异值的事件分解来控制随机搜索方向的扰动。在目标函数和约束函数二阶导数利普希茨连续的假设下，该方法达到随机ε-KKT点的期望总预言复杂度为O(ε⁻³)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机一阶方法在处理目标和约束均含随机性的非凸等式约束优化问题时，预言复杂度较高，尤其是随机约束函数评估次数过多。本文旨在降低这种复杂度。

Method: 提出基于分解随机搜索方向的随机一阶方法；采用Fletcher增广拉格朗日函数作为平滑价值函数用于步长选择；引入基于最小奇异值的事件分解机制以应对随机雅可比矩阵可能失去一致非退化性的问题。

Result: 在目标函数和约束函数二阶导数满足利普希茨连续的假设下，算法达到随机ε-KKT点的期望总预言复杂度为O(ε⁻³)。

Conclusion: 所提方法在理论上降低了预言复杂度，数值实验验证了其性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems in which only stochastic first-order approximations of the objective and constraint functions are available. Owing to the stochasticity in both objective and constraints, most existing stochastic first-order methods incur relatively high oracle complexity, particularly in terms of stochastic constraint function evaluations. To address this issue, we develop a stochastic first-order method based on a decomposed stochastic search direction, and employ Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian as a smooth merit function for step-size selection. To cope with the possible loss of uniform nondegeneracy of the stochastic Jacobian, we introduce an event decomposition based on the smallest singular value, which enables us to control perturbations in the stochastic search direction. Under an additional Lipschitz continuity assumption on the second-order derivatives of the objective and constraint functions, we show that the proposed algorithm attains a stochastic \(ε\)-KKT point with an expected total oracle complexity of \(\mathcal O(ε^{-3})\) in terms of stochastic gradient and stochastic constraint function evaluations. Finally, we present numerical experiments to demonstrate the performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [147] [Graphon Mean Field Game of mutual holding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28254)
*Daorong Cui,Shuoqing Deng,Yang Xiang*

Main category: math.OC
