TL;DR: 本文研究基于图论（graphon）的相互持有平均场博弈，在扩充空间框架下给出了最优策略的显式刻画、McKean-Vlasov SDE的适定性以及Nash均衡的收敛性结果，关键技术是对$\mathcal{WOP}_2$度量下连续性的精细分析及针对不同图论正则性的定制化论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Djete与Touzi(2024)提出的相互持有平均场博弈推广到图论（graphon）交互结构，以刻画更现实的不对称、异质主体间的相互作用。

Method: 采用Lacker与Soret(2023)的扩充空间框架，利用值过程与图论标签的联合分布建模；通过对$\mathcal{WOP}_2$度量下连续性性质的精细分析，结合针对不同图论正则性的定制化论证，推导最优策略显式形式、证明McKean-Vlasov SDE的适定性并建立均衡收敛性。

Result: 在图论函数满足适当条件下，获得了最优策略的显式刻画，证明了相关McKean-Vlasov SDE的适定性，并建立了Nash均衡的收敛结果。

Conclusion: 成功将相互持有平均场博弈推广至图论交互结构，提供了完整的理论分析框架。

Abstract: This paper studies the mean field game of mutual holding proposed by Djete and Touzi(AAP, 2024), and consider the case where the interactions among agents are described by a graphon. We adopt the formulation on the enlarged space which is modeled using the joint law of the value process and the graphon label, as in Lacker and Soret(MOR, 2023). Under suitable conditions on the graphon function, we are able to provide the explicit characterization of the optimal strategy, prove the wellposedness of associated Mckean-Vlasov SDE and establish the convergence results of the Nash equilibria. The key technique consists in a detailed analysis of the continuity property under the $\mathcal{WOP}_2$ metric, and tailor-made arguments for different graphon equilibria under different regularities of the model.

</details>


### [148] [Sharp First-Order Lower Bounds under Sublevel $α$-Polyak-Lojasiewicz Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28278)
*Saeed Masiha,Negar Kiyavash,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在α-Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件（α∈[1,2)）下一阶方法的最优复杂度。首先证明全局光滑与全局α-PL条件无法共存（α<2时函数必为常数），因此提出子水平集α-PL类。在该类上，证明了确定性一阶方法的minimax下界为Ω(Lτ^{2/α}ε^{-(2-α)/α})，匹配梯度下降；随机一阶方法在噪声主导区间的下界为Ω(Lσ^2τ^{4/α}ε^{-(4-α)/α})，匹配SGD上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-PL条件（α∈[1,2)）涵盖从Holder误差界（α=1）到经典PL条件（α=2）之间的广泛非凸优化场景，尤其出现在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz几何中退化极小值附近。此前缺乏对该条件下最优一阶方法复杂度的精确刻画，特别是当α<2时全局光滑与全局PL不等式存在结构性障碍未被揭示。

Method: 首先通过反例证明全局光滑+全局α-PL（α<2）导致函数恒为常数的结构性障碍。由此定义"全局光滑、子水平集α-PL"函数类，即PL不等式仅需在初始子水平集上成立。在此基础上，利用信息论下界技术（构造困难函数族、结合Fano不等式或Lecam方法）推导确定性及随机一阶oracle模型下的minimax下界。

Result: 确定性情形：下界Ω(Lτ^{2/α}ε^{-(2-α)/α})，与梯度下降匹配。随机（有界方差）情形：噪声主导区下界Ω(Lσ^2τ^{4/α}ε^{-(4-α)/α})，与SGD在轨迹有界假设下的已知上界匹配。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了α-PL条件下（α∈[1,2)）一阶方法的最优复杂度，揭示了全局光滑与全局α-PL在α<2时的不兼容性，并证明了梯度下降/SGD在该类上的最优性。

Abstract: We study the optimal complexity of first-order methods under the $α$-Polyak-Lojasiewicz condition with $α\in[1,2)$. This condition bounds the suboptimality gap by a power $α$ of the gradient norm; $α=2$ recovers the classical Polyak-Lojasiewicz condition, $α=1$ corresponds to a Holder error-bound regime, and intermediate exponents arise near degenerate minima in local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz geometry. We first prove a structural obstruction: if global smoothness and a global $α$-Polyak-Lojasiewicz inequality are imposed on $\mathbb{R}^d$, then every such function is constant for $α<2$. This motivates the globally smooth, sublevel-$α$-Polyak-Lojasiewicz class, where the inequality is required only on the initial sublevel set.
  On this class, we prove sharp minimax lower bounds for first-order methods. In the deterministic oracle model, any first-order method requires $Ω(Lτ^{2/α}ε^{-(2-α)/α})$ queries to reach accuracy $ε$, matching gradient descent. In the bounded-variance stochastic-gradient oracle model, any stochastic first-order method requires $Ω(Lσ^2τ^{4/α}ε^{-(4-α)/α})$ queries in the noise-dominated regime, matching known SGD upper rates under trajectory-containment assumptions.

</details>


### [149] [Second-Order KKT Guarantees for Bregman ADMM in Nonconvex and Non-Lipschitz Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28307)
*Shuang Li,Zhihui Zhu,Qiuwei Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了非凸线性约束问题在双侧相对光滑条件下的Bregman ADMM算法，证明其从随机初始化出发几乎必然收敛到严格鞍点（满足二阶平稳性），并扩展至分布式多块星形共识优化，通过数值实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸ADMM分析通常依赖全局Lipschitz梯度假设，但矩阵/张量模型中多项式目标函数常不满足该条件。本文旨在建立更宽松的"双侧相对光滑"框架下的Bregman ADMM收敛性理论。

Method: 1) 构造光滑原始-对偶不动点映射，证明严格鞍点KKT点是不稳定不动点；2) 采用Bregman特定对称化与缩放技巧进行行列式约减，结合两块谱论证；3) 针对星形共识结构，利用零空间消去技术扩展至多块分布式优化。

Result: 1) 从随机初始化出发，迭代以概率1避开严格鞍点；2) 结合一阶收敛结果，几乎必然达到极限KKT点的二阶平稳性；3) 在分布式矩阵分解和张量分解上验证了理论有效性。

Conclusion: Bregman ADMM在双侧相对光滑的非凸问题中能几乎必然收敛到二阶临界点，为处理无全局Lipschitz梯度的矩阵/张量优化提供了理论保证，且可扩展至分布式共识场景。

Abstract: We analyze Bregman ADMM for nonconvex linearly constrained problems under two-sided relative smoothness, a condition that replaces the standard Lipschitz gradient assumption with a Hessian comparison relative to a Bregman kernel. This setting covers polynomial objectives arising in matrix and tensor models for which a global Lipschitz-gradient constant need not exist. We show that on an invariant open state-space domain, one iteration of Bregman ADMM defines a smooth primal--dual fixed-point map whose strict-saddle KKT points are unstable fixed points; consequently, from random initialization the iterates converge to a strict saddle with probability zero. Combined with existing first-order convergence results, this yields almost-sure second-order stationarity of limiting KKT points. We extend the analysis to a multi-block star consensus formulation for distributed optimization. The technical novelty lies in a determinant reduction with a Bregman-specific symmetrization and scaling step in the two block spectral argument, together with a null space cancellation exploiting the star graph structure in the consensus case. Numerical experiments on distributed matrix factorization illustrate the theory, and a symmetric tensor factorization example demonstrates the broader Bregman proximal splitting idea beyond the separable consensus setting.

</details>
